Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要在英语中开发，假设其能跨语言泛化，但这一假设在低资源语言中未得到充分验证，可能构成安全漏洞。作者旨在检验这种假设是否成立。

Method: 1) 构建LoDNA安全数据集，包含字面翻译和文化本地化提示两种形式；2) 提出潜在几何框架，通过探测LLM隐状态中的拒绝表示来评估跨语言安全迁移，超越传统的基于生成的评估方法。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限，大多数语言-模型组合中有害提示保留的英语拒绝信号不足10%。字面翻译与本地化提示在语义上高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但在各层之间存在漂移，表明模型编码了概念但未将其路由到安全机制。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是表面化的，研究结果为"存在通用、语言无关的危害流形"这一假设提供了强有力的反证，至少在所研究的低资源语言范围内如此。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综合分析了TrustNLP工作坊自2021年以来六届共144篇论文，提出了六大信任维度的分类框架，揭示了从静态模型的事后可解释性向生成系统的机制理解和主动控制的范式转变，并分析了信任维度与能力涌现的共现关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI系统的快速发展，对NLP系统可信性的研究日益重要，但缺乏系统性的综述来梳理该领域的演进脉络、主题分布和未来方向。

Method: 对TrustNLP全部144篇论文进行系统分析，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等既有框架提出六维信任分类体系，并进行跨会议对比（ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP约2000篇论文）以检验TrustNLP主题分布的领域代表性。

Result: 研究发现：聊天模型的发布同时激活了所有信任维度；真实性是最快增长的维度（从2021-2022年的缺失到2025-2026年占37%）；公平性是最持续的主题；可解释性呈现U形轨迹（事后方法的衰落与机制可解释性的复兴）；TrustNLP的主题分布与领域平均水平高度一致；识别出四个结构性洞见。

Conclusion: 该领域已从"理解模型"转向"控制生成系统"，作者为研究社区提供了可操作的未来研究方向。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG框架，用于生成符合CPS（儿童保护服务）对齐的合成VAWG（针对妇女和女孩的暴力）多轮对话数据集。该框架从人物种子、英国人口统计数据、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀案审查案例构建场景，生成层级事件时间线，并产出多场景角色扮演对话，同时通过激活引导的毒性控制来调节话语。最终发布了200个场景、6000多个多轮对话事件的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏感领域（如家庭暴力）的真实对话数据难以获取、发布和标注，受隐私和法律限制。现有研究多聚焦于句子级在线毒性检测，缺乏对虐待作为关系性和随时间展开现象的建模，因此需要合成方法来生成多轮对话级的数据。

Method: ConVAWG采用检索增强框架：(1) 从人物种子、ONS人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索的Domestic Homicide Review案例构建场景；(2) 将场景转化为层级事件时间线；(3) 生成多场景角色扮演对话；(4) 应用激活引导（activation-steering）的毒性控制到适当话语上。

Result: 生成了200个场景、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，带有丰富的场景级、事件级和回合级元数据。通过广泛的人工评估、LLM作为裁判评估、消融实验和下游任务验证，展示了强劲的对话质量和领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG填补了VAWG多轮对话建模的空白，证明了通过检索增强和受控生成可以合成高质量、领域保真的敏感对话数据，为后续研究和下游任务提供支撑。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Sequential Modality Dropout for Robust Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10240)
*Guanqun Yang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出顺序模态丢弃（SMD）训练方法：训练时以概率p独立擦除每个模态流（图像/文本）的整个用户交互历史，使多模态顺序推荐器学会在缺少任何单一模态时仍能准确预测下一项。在四个骨干模型和四个Amazon数据集上，SMD将文本保留率提升1.0-3.2倍，且几乎不损失完整模态精度；在95%的逐项缺失极端情况下，HR@10保留率从22%提升至61%（2.8倍改进）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实商品目录常缺失图像或文本等模态，但现有模型假设每个商品都携带所有模态。在完整数据上训练的模型在服务时某模态缺失会显著丧失推荐精度，需要一种使模型对缺失模态具备鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出Sequence Modality Dropout（SMD）：训练时每个模态流（图像和文本）以概率p为整个用户交互历史独立擦除，迫使模型学习不依赖任何单一模态进行下一项预测。这是一种四行代码、与架构无关的改动，并可叠加可选的跨模态重建损失以进一步增强严重缺失下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个骨干模型（MM-SASRec、IISAN、MISSRec、fMRLRec）和四个Amazon域上，SMD将文本保留率提升1.0-3.2倍，且几乎不损失完整模态精度；在95%的极端缺失率下，HR@10保留率从22%提升至61%（2.8倍改进）；可选跨模态重建损失在严重文本缺失下将简单加性骨干的保留率从90%提升至98%。

Conclusion: SMD是一种简单（四行代码）、架构无关的训练时改动，能有效使多模态顺序推荐器在实际部署中遇到的模态缺失场景下保持鲁棒性，同时几乎不影响完整模态下的性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal sequential recommenders assume every item carries every modality, but real product catalogs often miss images or text, and a model trained on complete data loses much of its recommendation accuracy when a modality is unavailable at serving time. We propose Sequential Modality Dropout (SMD): during training, each modality stream (image and text) is independently erased with probability p for an entire user interaction history, so the model learns to predict the next item without relying on any single modality. We measure robustness by retention, the fraction of a model's full-modality accuracy (HR@10) that survives when a modality is removed at test time. Across four backbones (MM-SASRec, IISAN, MISSRec, and fMRLRec) on four Amazon domains, SMD raises text retention by 1.0 to 3.2x at essentially no cost to full-modality accuracy; under an extreme 95% per-item missing rate, it retains 61% of HR@10 versus 22% without (a 2.8x improvement). An optional cross-modal reconstruction loss further lifts retention from 90% to 98% on a simple additive backbone under severe text missingness. SMD is a four-line, architecture-agnostic change that makes multi-modal sequential recommenders robust to the missing modalities they actually encounter in deployment.

</details>


### [177] [Do LLM Recommenders Know When They're Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10008)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对四种零样本LLM推荐系统（Mistral Large、Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Sonnet 4.6）在三个目录（MovieLens-25M、Amazon Toys、Yelp）上进行幻觉率与置信度校准的联合审计。发现幻觉率高度依赖目录（0-8.3%），但所有模型的言语化置信度系统性欠置信（即使在零幻觉时ECE高达0.223）。这种欠置信源于"直接问询"引发的提示不匹配，导致基于置信度的弃权阈值无法有效区分正确项与幻觉项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对LLM推荐器的审计只关注二元的目录外（OOD）幻觉率，从未检验模型是否知道自己在幻觉（即置信度校准问题），因此无法判断置信度信号是否能用于检测和缓解幻觉。

Method: 对四个不同厂商的零样本LLM推荐器在三个目录上，按物品流行度分层，联合测量OOD@10幻觉率和言语化置信度校准（ECE、Brier、可靠性图），并测试基于置信度的conformal弃权阈值在多个alpha水平下的效果。

Result: 幻觉率因目录而异（MovieLens 0-0.2%，Amazon 4.5-8.3%，Yelp 2.2-8.4%）；所有模型在所有12个单元中系统性欠置信（推荐准确率92-100%却只表达67-86的置信度）；conformal弃权阈值最多降低0.7个百分点幻觉率，却付出4-21个百分点的覆盖率代价。

Conclusion: LLM推荐器审计应同时报告校准与OOD；应使用目录锚定的提示（catalog-anchored elicitation）而非通用置信度提示来获取更可靠的置信度信号；欠置信现象提示"Just Ask"式置信度获取方式存在本质性的提示不匹配问题。

Abstract: LLM recommenders for top-$K$ item suggestion regularly emit titles outside the target catalog. Prior audits measure this as a binary out-of-domain rate; none ask whether the model knew it was hallucinating. We jointly audit hallucination rate (OOD@10) and verbalized-confidence calibration (ECE, Brier, reliability) for four zero-shot LLM recommenders from four independent vendors (Mistral Large, Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-OSS-120B, Claude Sonnet 4.6), not grounded or fine-tuned systems, across three catalogs (MovieLens-25M, Amazon Reviews 2023 Toys, Yelp Open Dataset), stratified by item popularity. Hallucination is catalog-dependent (0--0.2\% on MovieLens, 4.5--8.3\% on Amazon, 2.2--8.4\% on Yelp), but verbalized confidence is materially miscalibrated even when hallucination is zero (ECE up to 0.223 on MovieLens despite 0\% OOD). All four LLMs are systematically \emph{under}-confident across all twelve cells, verbalizing a mean of 67--86 on items they recommend with 92--100\% accuracy. This is the opposite of the over-confidence usually emphasized in LLM-hallucination work. The under-confidence is best read as an \emph{elicitation mismatch}: ``Just Ask'' elicits a generic recommendation-quality rating, not a catalog-membership probability. A conformal abstention threshold over verbalized confidence reduces hallucination by at most 0.7\,pp across $α\in \{.05, .10, .15, .20\}$, at 4--21\,pp of coverage cost: the under-confident channel cannot separate correct items from hallucinations, so the threshold mostly removes correct items. We recommend that audits of LLM recommenders report calibration alongside OOD, and use catalog-anchored elicitation rather than generic confidence prompts.

</details>


### [178] [ConnectionMind: Leveraging Social Networks and Large Language Models for Personalized Recommendation at Meta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10187)
*Haoyu Han,Yuming Liu,Lei Huang,Lizhu Zhang,Jiliang Tang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ConnectionMind是Meta提出的生产级推荐框架，将社交网络图结构与LLM相结合，把推荐建模为图推理问题，通过两阶段训练（SFT+RL）实现可扩展、可解释且具备推理能力的个性化推荐，在线A/B测试中视频观看时长提升0.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐模型要么忽略社交关系信号，要么将其独立处理，缺乏整合多关系上下文（好友、群组、创作者互动等）进行细粒度个性化的推理能力，难以应对大规模异构内容与复杂社交关系的建模需求。

Method: 构建连接用户、商品、好友、群组和创作者页面的异构图，将推荐形式化为图推理问题（发现从用户到候选物品的个性化路径）；采用基于LLM的策略在图结构上进行推理以指导推荐决策；采用两阶段训练策略：先通过大规模用户-物品交互轨迹进行SFT初始化推理策略，再通过端到端RL优化模型在社交图上推理的能力。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验证明了相比代表性基线的有效性；已部署于Meta大规模推荐管线，在线A/B测试实现视频观看时长0.43%的提升，展示了在生产环境中的可衡量的实际影响。

Conclusion: ConnectionMind证明了将社交图结构与LLM推理能力深度整合可以在生产级推荐系统中实现具有可解释性和推理能力的个性化，并在真实业务指标上验证了其实际价值。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems on social media platforms such as Meta must model complex social relationships, including friendships, group memberships, and creator interactions, alongside massive and heterogeneous content such as text and video. Traditional recommendation models, however, often omit these signals or treat them independently, lacking the reasoning capability to integrate multi-relational context for fine-grained personalization. We present ConnectionMind, a production-ready recommendation framework that tightly integrates the social network structure with large language models (LLMs) to enable scalable, interpretable, and reasoning-aware personalization in Meta. ConnectionMind constructs a heterogeneous graph connecting users, items, friends, groups, and creator pages, and formulates recommendation as a graph reasoning problem: discovering personalized paths from users to candidate items. An LLM-based policy is employed to reason over these graph structures and guide recommendation decisions. To train the system at scale, ConnectionMind adopts a two-stage learning strategy. We first perform supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on large-scale user-item interaction trajectories to initialize the reasoning policy, followed by end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) to refine the model's ability to reason over social graphs for personalized recommendation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ConnectionMind compared to representative baselines. More importantly, ConnectionMind has been deployed in Meta's large-scale recommendation pipeline and has been evaluated through online A/B tests, achieving a 0.43% improvement in video watch time. These results demonstrate measurable real-world impact in a production recommendation system.

</details>


### [179] [DualSpectralCF: Training-Free Sign-Aware Spectral Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10247)
*Guanqun Yang,Tong Qi,Xiaoxue Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DualSpectralCF 是一个免训练的谱协同过滤框架，通过引入显式负反馈（不喜欢信号）来增强现有的谱骨干方法（如 ChebyCF、GF-CF、Turbo-CF）。它包含两个组件：带符号的输入信号和带符号的物品-物品算子，仅需两个标量超参数。在5个基准数据集上，所有实例都匹配或超越其无符号骨干方法，Recall@20提升最高达+32.6%，并且比SIGformer快7.7到155.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的sign-aware推荐器只能通过基于梯度的训练来利用显式负反馈，成本高昂；而训练免费的谱协同过滤方法虽然高效但只处理正向交互。论文希望弥合这两条研究路线之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出DualSpectralCF框架，包含两个组件：(1) 编码用户显式不喜欢的带符号输入信号 r_u^±；(2) 融合"喜欢者相似"和"不喜欢者相似"的带符号物品-物品算子 M^±。该框架与骨干方法无关，仅增加两个标量超参数 (γ, κ)。

Result: 在5个sign-aware基准数据集上，所有DualSpectralCF实例均匹配或超越其无符号骨干版本，Recall@20最高提升+32.6%（骨干特定调参）和+1.9%至+16.0%（固定默认参数）。相比SIGformer快7.7到155.3倍，达到其70.7%到90.7%的精度。冷启动用户获益最大，Epinions上1-5个训练物品的用户Recall@20提升高达+29.2%。

Conclusion: 显式负反馈可以通过训练免费的方式有效融入谱协同过滤，实现精度与效率兼得。DualSpectralCF为sign-aware推荐提供了一个高效的免训练替代方案，尤其在冷启动场景下表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Real-world recommendation platforms routinely collect explicit negative feedback such as 1-star reviews, hate-button clicks, distrust between users, and very-low watch-ratio videos. Learned sign-aware recommenders exploit this signal for clear accuracy gains, but only at the cost of gradient-based training. In parallel, a line of training-free spectral collaborative filtering methods matches or beats learned graph recommenders at a fraction of the cost, yet operates on positive interactions alone. We bridge these two lines with DualSpectralCF, a training-free framework of two components that attach to any spectral backbone of the form $\hat{\mathbf{r}}_u = F(\mathbf{M}) \mathbf{r}_u$: a signed input signal $\mathbf{r}_u^{\pm}$ that encodes the user's explicit dislikes, and a signed item-item operator $\mathbf{M}^{\pm}$ that blends like-together and dislike-together similarity. The framework is backbone-agnostic and adds just two scalar hyperparameters. We instantiate DualSpectralCF on ChebyCF, GF-CF, and Turbo-CF, and evaluate on five sign-aware benchmarks: every instance matches or beats its unsigned backbone on all 5 datasets, with Recall@20 lifts up to +32.6% with backbone-specific $(γ, κ)$ tuning and +1.9% to +16.0% for DualSpectralCF-Cheby at the fixed default $(γ= -0.5, κ= 0.1)$, and the family runs 7.7 to 155.3$\times$ faster than SIGformer while reaching 70.7% to 90.7% of its accuracy. Sign-awareness helps most for cold-start users, with up to +29.2% Recall@20 on Epinions users with 1 to 5 training items.

</details>


### [180] [GenRec: An LLM-Backed Recommendation Ranker at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10257)
*Ying Li,Shradha Sehgal,Arjun Rao,Rein Houthooft,Yaochen Zhu,Ashish Rastogi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Netflix探索的GenRec系统——一个基于内部基础LLM的推荐排序器。GenRec采用两阶段框架：第一阶段将开源LLM适配到Netflix数据；第二阶段（本文重点）用推荐排序相关数据进行后训练，从传统的数千特征判别式排序器转向由语言化用户历史和上下文驱动的LLM排序器。大规模A/B测试表明，GenRec用更少的第二阶段标注样本和输入信号即可在离线和在线指标上取得统计显著提升，并讨论了从特征工程到上下文工程、从专用架构到共享基础骨干的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖大量人工设计的特征和专用架构，难以充分建模用户、内容和上下文之间的复杂语义关系。LLM提供了直接用自然语言建模这些维度的能力，但在实际生产系统中如何将LLM应用于推荐排序仍缺乏系统性验证。

Method: GenRec采用两阶段框架：Phase 1将开源LLM适配到Netflix数据，建立目录和会员行为的深层理解；Phase 2（本文核心）用推荐排序特定数据、标签和奖励信号进行后训练。设计包括输入语言化和上下文工程、后训练数据构建、奖励整合、模型架构，以及基于prefill-only推理的成本受限服务方案。

Result: 大规模A/B测试显示，GenRec用显著更少的Phase-2标注训练样本和输入信号，即可在离线和在线指标上取得相比当前生产排序器的统计显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的推荐系统正在改变推荐范式：从特征工程转向上下文工程，从专用架构转向共享基础骨干。同时在实际资源约束下服务此类系统需要精心设计推理成本和工程方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping recommender systems by enabling richer modeling of users, content, and context directly in natural language. At Netflix, we are exploring this direction through GenRec, an LLM-backed recommendation ranker built on top of an in-house foundational LLM. GenRec follows a two-phase framework: Phase 1 adapts an open-source LLM to Netflix data, developing deep understanding of the catalog and member behavior while balancing capabilities such as content understanding and instruction following. Phase 2 post-trains this foundation model with recommendation-ranking specific data, labels, and reward signals, aiming to align the ranker with business requirements and long-term member satisfaction.
  This paper focuses on Phase 2 and the transition from a traditional discriminative ranker with thousands of engineered features to an LLM-backed ranker driven by verbalized user histories and context. We describe our design for input verbalization and context engineering, post-training data construction, reward integration, model architecture, and a cost-constrained serving design based on a prefill-only inference approach. We report results from a large-scale A/B test comparing GenRec against the current production ranker model, where we show that a GenRec model trained with substantially fewer Phase-2 labeled training examples and input signals can achieve statistically significant gains in offline and online metrics. We discuss how LLM-backed recommenders could shift the recommendation paradigm: from feature engineering to context engineering, and from bespoke architectures to shared foundation backbones. We also outline practical lessons for serving such systems under real-world resource constraints.

</details>


### [181] [Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering: Rethinking Graph Collaborative Filtering as Tree Collaborative Filtering with Curvature-Aware Propagation Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10297)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出神经树协同过滤（NTCF）框架，从树结构视角重新审视图协同过滤（GCF），通过基于局部度不平衡分数的闭式解（作为离散Ricci曲率代理）为每个节点分配个性化的传播深度，解决了传统GCF中均匀传播深度导致的外围节点过平滑和枢纽节点探索不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCF方法对所有节点使用统一的传播层数，忽略了真实交互图中节点局部连通性的显著差异。这导致外围节点（低连接度）在深层传播中迅速遭受过平滑问题，而枢纽节点（高连接度）则无法超越其直接邻域被充分探索。这种一刀切的传播策略限制了GCF的表达能力和推荐性能。

Method: NTCF将每个节点的局部邻域重新解释为有根树，基于一个闭式局部度不平衡分数（作为离散Ricci曲率代理）为每个节点分配特定的传播深度。该框架从理论上证明了：(i) NTCF严格泛化NGCF，当所有曲率诱导的深度调整消失时退化为NGCF；(ii) 曲率感知的传播调度在正曲率（外围）节点的深层保留的判别信息严格多于均匀深度传播。

Result: NTCF在三个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，其性能超过大多数广泛使用的GCF骨干模型，并且可以作为骨干集成到现有的先进自监督模型中，替换原始骨干后实现性能提升。

Conclusion: NTCF通过曲率感知的节点特定传播深度解决了GCF中均匀传播深度忽视节点局部结构差异的缺陷，既具有理论保证又具备实际性能优势，是一种可广泛替代现有GCF骨干的通用框架。

Abstract: Graph Collaborative Filtering (GCF) has become the dominant paradigm in modern recommender systems by modeling user-item interactions as a bipartite graph and propagating embeddings through a fixed number of message-passing layers. However, applying a uniform propagation depth to every node ignores a fundamental property of real interaction graphs: nodes differ substantially in their local connectivity, so peripheral nodes quickly suffer from over-smoothing while hub-like nodes remain under-explored beyond their immediate neighborhood. In this paper, we revisit GCF from a tree-structured perspective and propose Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering (NTCF), a framework that re-interprets each node's local neighborhood as a rooted tree and assigns a node-specific propagation depth based on a closed-form local-degree-imbalance score that serves as a discrete Ricci-curvature proxy. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that (i) NTCF strictly generalizes NGCF, degenerating to NGCF when all curvature-induced depth adjustments vanish (a lower bound on its representation power), and (ii) the curvature-aware schedule retains strictly more discriminative information at deep layers on positively-curved (peripheral) nodes than uniform-depth propagation. NTCF can achieve higher performance than most widely used GCF backbone models and can be integrated into existing advanced self-supervised models as a backbone, replacing their original backbone to achieve enhanced performance. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of NTCF.

</details>


### [182] [Persona Conditioning as an Assessor-Sensitivity Probe for LLM-Based IR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10385)
*Samaneh Mohtadi,Pietro Bernardelle,Joel Mackenzie,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了人物角色条件化（persona conditioning）如何影响LLM作为相关性评估者的判断可靠性。通过从PersonaHub和NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA两个来源构建五种评估者角色，在六个LLM骨干模型上于TREC DL20和RAG24数据集上进行实验，发现评估者敏感性是结构化的而非均匀的：判断通常接近基线但会转移严格度或证据阈值，高容量模型保持系统排名一致性而小模型放大不稳定性，且敏感性集中在特定类型的检索系统上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作IR评估中的相关性评估者，但评估者框架（persona conditioning）如何影响判断可靠性和下游系统比较尚未被系统研究。作者希望将人物角色条件化作为诊断机制，揭示LLM评估者的敏感性及其对评估结果的影响。

Method: 使用两种互补来源（PersonaHub和NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA）的人物角色，实例化五种评估者角色：意图解释、领域专长、对比判断、证据验证和全局搜索质量评估，并与标准UMBRELA基线对比。在六个LLM骨干模型上，于TREC DL20和RAG24两个数据集上进行实验，结合系统级排名一致性和局部排名位移分析。

Result: 发现评估者敏感性是结构化的而非均匀的：判断通常接近基线但仍会转移评估严格度、证据阈值或解释重点，而非产生大规模相关性反转。高容量模型保持系统排名一致性，而小模型放大人物角色引发的不稳定性。局部排名位移分析显示敏感性集中在特定检索系统上，DL20上集中于神经排序/重排序系统，RAG24上集中于RAG导向管道。人物角色来源的影响小于评估者角色和模型容量。

Conclusion: 人物角色条件化判断可作为受控的敏感性探针，用于压力测试基于LLM的IR评估管道，并识别出那些评估结果对评估者框架敏感的系统。这些发现对LLM评估器的可靠部署和IR评估方法的稳健性设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as relevance assessors in information retrieval (IR) evaluation, raising questions about how assessor framing affects judgment reliability and downstream system comparison. We study persona conditioning as a diagnostic mechanism for exposing LLM assessor sensitivity. Using task-oriented personas drawn from two complementary sources (PersonaHub and NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA), we instantiate five assessor roles emphasizing intent interpretation, domain expertise, contrastive judgment, evidence verification, and global search-quality assessment, compared with a standard UMBRELA baseline. Across six LLM backbones on TREC DL20 and RAG24, our analyses reveal structured rather than uniform assessor sensitivity. Judgments usually remain close to the baseline while shifting assessment strictness, evidential threshold, or interpretation emphasis rather than producing widespread relevance reversals. At the system level, high-capacity models preserve system-ranking agreement, while smaller models amplify persona-induced instability. Local rank-displacement analysis shows sensitivity concentrates on particular retrieval systems and system types, especially neural ranking/reranking systems on DL20 and RAG-oriented pipelines on RAG24. Persona source matters less than assessor role and model capacity. These findings position persona-conditioned judging as a controlled sensitivity probe for stress-testing LLM-based IR evaluation pipelines and identifying systems whose evaluation outcomes are sensitive to assessor framing.

</details>


### [183] [Post-Calibration Reliability Reranking of Relevance Decisions via Label-wise Monotone Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10406)
*Inwoo Tae,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出标签单调可靠性投影（MRP）方法，学习标签单调函数将校准后的置信度映射为正确性可靠性，保留原始预测标签和类别概率，从而根据残差风险对固定预测进行重排序。在六个信息访问相关数据集上，MRP提升了可靠性重排序和平均回退效用，同时保持了全覆盖准确率和ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后处理校准仅使置信度与平均正确率对齐，无法消除同一校准置信度水平内依赖预测标签的可靠性差异，导致系统过度信任错误预测或不必要地推迟正确预测。

Method: 提出标签单调可靠性投影（MRP），学习标签单调函数，将校准后的置信度映射到正确性可靠性，同时保留原始预测标签和类别概率，利用残差风险对固定预测进行重排序。

Result: 在六个信息访问相关性数据集和多个后处理校准器上，MRP提升了可靠性重排序和平均回退效用，同时保持全覆盖准确率和ECE。消融实验表明主要增益来自标签相关的残差可靠性而非全局置信度重映射。

Conclusion: MRP能有效解决校准后仍存在的标签依赖可靠性差异问题，可靠性重排序是主要目标，而将可靠性分数嵌入顶层标签概率几何空间的分析可作为兼容性分析，但与主目标不同。

Abstract: Web search, product search, and question-answering retrieval systems often assign a relevance label and confidence score to each query-candidate pair. The relevance label describes how well a page, product, or passage matches the query, while the confidence often guides downstream use or fallback decisions. Post-hoc calibration is therefore needed because misaligned confidence can make systems over-trust wrong predictions or unnecessarily defer correct ones. However, calibration mainly aligns confidence with average correctness, and does not remove predicted-label-dependent reliability differences that remain within the same calibrated confidence level. We address this gap with Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP), which learns label-wise monotone functions that map calibrated confidence to correctness reliability while preserving the original predicted labels and class probabilities. The resulting reliability score reranks fixed predictions according to residual risk. Across six information access relevance datasets and multiple post-hoc calibrators, MRP improves reliability reranking and average fallback utility while preserving full-coverage accuracy and ECE. Structural ablations show that the main gains come from label-wise residual reliability rather than from global confidence remapping. We further analyze when MRP reliability scores can be embedded back into top-label probability geometry, showing that this projection is useful as a compatibility analysis but is distinct from the main reliability-reranking objective. The implementation will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [184] [Towards Efficient Reasoning in LLM-Based Recommender Systems via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10447)
*Linh Dieu Le,Tong Chen,Shazia Sadiq,Hongzhi Yin,Ming Jin,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REAM，首个用于推荐系统中推理压缩的模型合并框架。与传统的均匀合并系数不同，REAM在注意力头级别进行细粒度合并，为每个注意力头分配不同的合并系数，将快思考模型的简洁行为选择性注入慢思考模型，在三个基准数据集上将推理长度减少24.3%的同时保持推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推荐系统中的慢思考模型虽然准确率更高，但推理轨迹冗长，增加推理成本且准确率增益有限。现有基于训练的推理压缩方法适配成本高，而推理时方法脆弱且难以扩展，亟需一种训练免费且可扩展的推理压缩方案。

Method: 提出REAM模型合并框架：在共享参数空间中，将慢思考模型与快思考模型进行注意力头级别的细粒度合并。每个注意力头根据其对关键推理证据的贡献度及对参数变化的敏感性，分配独立的合并系数，从而选择性将快思考模型的简洁行为注入慢思考模型，在降低冗长度同时保持推荐质量。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法将推理长度减少最高24.3%，同时在保持推荐准确率方面优于竞争性的模型合并基线。

Conclusion: 通过在注意力头级别进行细粒度模型合并，能够有效实现推理压缩与推荐准确率之间的平衡，为推荐系统中的推理压缩提供了训练免费的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model-based recommender systems are increasingly adopting slow-thinking models that generate step-by-step reasoning before making predictions, often achieving higher accuracy than fast-thinking models that predict directly. However, their reasoning traces are often unnecessarily verbose, increasing inference costs without commensurate accuracy gains. Existing training-based approaches to reasoning compression often incur substantial adaptation costs, while inference-time methods are brittle and difficult to scale. These limitations motivate model merging as a promising training-free direction for transferring specialised behaviours between models in a shared parameter space. In particular, merging a slow-thinking model with a fast-thinking counterpart provides a natural mechanism for balancing recommendation accuracy and reasoning conciseness. To this end, we propose, to our knowledge, the first model merging framework for reasoning compression in recommender systems. Unlike conventional merging methods that apply uniform merge coefficients across model components, our method performs fine-grained merging at the level of individual attention heads, capturing heterogeneous patterns in recommendation reasoning. Each attention head is assigned a distinct merge coefficient according to its contribution to critical reasoning evidence and its sensitivity to parameter change, enabling selective injection of the concise behaviour of the fast-thinking model into the slow-thinking model and reducing reasoning verbosity without compromising recommendation quality. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that our method reduces reasoning length by up to 24.3% while outperforming competitive model merging baselines in maintaining recommendation accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linhledieu/REAM.

</details>


### [185] [When Do Anchor-Based Pointwise LLM Rerankers Help? Retriever Quality, Statistical Scope, and Anchor Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10528)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文以可复现性为先的研究方式，对基于锚点的逐点式LLM重排序方法（以GCCP/PAGC为代表）进行了受控组件级压力测试。研究发现，仅凭论文文本复现只能达到0.24而非报告的0.66 nDCG@10，需要识别并恢复八个未记录的实现细节才能复现报告结果（误差在1.6%以内）。核心对比打分思想在严格统计校正下是稳健的，但两个原始论文中固定的设计选择并不可靠：对比分数与标准逐点相关分数的结合仅在BM25作为第一级检索器时有帮助；论文中复杂的锚点构建方法不如简单的句间交错法。这些发现跨不同LLM骨干（包括4-bit量化的72B模型）保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点式逐点LLM重排序声称能以逐点计算成本恢复跨文档上下文，但已有研究往往缺少严格的可复现性和组件级分析。作者希望弄清该方法在何种条件下真正有效，哪些设计选择贡献了实际增益，以及报告的结果是否可靠可复现。

Method: 采用"复现优先"的研究思路：首先基于论文文本进行初步复现，识别并恢复八个未记录的实现细节；然后在验证过的实现基础上，进行受控组件级压力测试，分别检验对比打分、分数组合策略、锚点构建方法在不同第一级检索器（BM25 vs E5）和不同LLM骨干上的表现。

Result: 初步复现仅达到0.24 nDCG@10（报告值为0.66），恢复八个未记录细节后复现结果在1.6%误差内。核心对比打分思想经统计校正后仍稳健；但对比分数与标准逐点分数的组合仅对BM25有帮助，对E5等强稠密检索器无增益；论文的复杂锚点构建不必要，简单的句间交错锚点在数据集上匹配或超越其表现。结论跨多个LLM骨干一致。

Conclusion: 锚点式逐点重排序确实有效，但其增益主要来自对比打分本身，而非复杂的聚合和锚点构建设计；且其有效性条件比原始论文评估所示的更窄。该研究强调了可复现性和组件级分析对评估检索方法真实价值的重要性。

Abstract: Anchor-based pointwise LLM reranking scores each candidate against a shared reference passage to recover cross-document context at pointwise cost. We study when this actually helps, using GCCP/PAGC as a representative method. Our study is reproduction-first. We use reproduction as a starting point for a controlled component-level stress test of anchor-based pointwise reranking. Our initial reimplementation, based only on the paper text, achieves 0.24 nDCG@10 instead of the reported 0.66, revealing that several undocumented implementation details are necessary to reproduce the method. After identifying and recovering eight such details, we reproduce the reported results within 1.6% and use the validated implementation for controlled analysis.
  We find that the core contrastive scoring idea is robust under rigorous statistical correction. However, two design choices held fixed in the original paper are less reliable. First, we find that combining the contrastive score with the standard pointwise relevance score helps when the first-stage retriever is BM25, but gives little or no benefit when the first-stage retriever is a stronger dense model such as E5. Second, the paper's more complex method for constructing the anchor is unnecessary. A much simpler anchor, built by interleaving the top-ranked sentences, matches or outperforms it across datasets. These findings are consistent across different LLM backbones, including a 4-bit quantized 72B model. Overall, anchor-based pointwise reranking is effective, but its gains come mainly from contrastive scoring rather than from the more complex aggregation and anchor-construction choices, and they appear under narrower conditions than the original evaluation suggests.

</details>


### [186] [Multi Interests for Joint Search-Recommendation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10535)
*Xiangchen Pan,Wei Wei,Huakang Niu,Zhicong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIJSR框架，从结构和语义两个角度对搜索-推荐混合序列进行多兴趣挖掘与自适应融合，并通过多任务预测提升搜索和推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域统一建模框架虽能弥补搜索与推荐行为域间差异，但忽略了混合序列中不同场景下兴趣表达的差异，缺乏细粒度的多兴趣建模。

Method: MIJSR包含三大模块：1) 通过对比学习对齐query与item表征实现跨域行为融合；2) 从结构角度（子序列划分与掩码设置提取搜索兴趣、推荐兴趣、交叉兴趣）和语义角度（query语义聚类进行语义分割构建语义多兴趣）进行多兴趣挖掘；3) 多兴趣自适应融合并结合侧信息，采用渐进式分层提取模型进行多任务预测。

Result: 在两个开源数据集上的大量实验表明，MIJSR通过细粒度提取用户多兴趣，能够进一步提升搜索和推荐任务的准确性。

Conclusion: MIJSR通过结构+语义双视角的多兴趣挖掘与自适应融合，有效提升了搜索推荐混合序列场景下的建模能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Search and recommendation are crucial for understanding user preferences. More and more studies are attempting to jointly model search behavior and recommendation behavior, by integrating user active search and passive recommendation behavior data to better mine user preferences. However, although existing cross-domain unified modeling frameworks can effectively compensate for the differences in behavior between domains, they overlook the expression of interests in different scenarios under mixed sequences. In this study, we propose a multi-interest-based mixed sequential modeling framework MIJSR, which performs multi-interest mining and adaptive integration on search recommendation mixed sequences from both structural and semantic perspectives. Specifically, our model can be roughly divided into three modules: cross-domain behavior fusion, multi-interest mining, and multi-task prediction. Firstly, we align the representations of query and item through contrastive learning training. Then, we extract the multi interests of the mixed behavior sequence from both structural and semantic perspectives. Structurally, we extract search interests, recommendation interests, and cross interests through subsequence partitioning and mask settings; In terms of semantics, we use the semantic information of queries for clustering and perform semantic segmentation on mixed sequences to construct semantic multi interests. Finally, the adaptive fusion of multiple interests is combined with other side information to use a progressive layered extraction model for multi-task prediction. Extensive experiments on two open-source datasets have shown that our model can further enhance its accuracy in search and recommendation by extracting users' multi interests at a fine-grained level. Codes are available at https://github.com/pxcstart/MIJSR.

</details>


### [187] [DistilVDR: A Compact End-to-End Visual Document Retriever via Dual-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10636)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Ziyi Wang,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DistilVDR,一个524M参数的端到端视觉文档检索(VDR)系统,通过从8B视觉语言教师模型的嵌入空间中蒸馏得到。该方法采用非对称编码器结构,在文档侧集中视觉能力(454M)、查询侧保持轻量(70M),通过逐点余弦对齐损失训练,无需相关性标签或负采样。DistilVDR-HiRes在ViDoRe基准上达到61.74平均NDCG@5(教师模型的86.9%),DistilVDR-Fast以3倍更小的视觉token预算达到59.98,同时索引大小比最强sub-1B多向量基线小15.6倍,索引速度提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索严重依赖数十亿参数的大模型,索引速度慢且部署成本高。先前的压缩方法要么从头训练小型多向量编码器,要么只蒸馏查询侧,均无法端到端地得到紧凑的单向量检索器。作者希望填补这一空白,构建一个既紧凑又能保持高性能的端到端单向量VDR系统。

Method: 1) 从单个8B视觉语言教师模型中通过逐点余弦对齐损失进行双侧蒸馏,所有监督信号来自冻结教师模型的嵌入空间(该空间本身经过相关监督训练),因此学生目标无需相关性标签、负采样或对比项。2) 采用非对称编码器结构:文档侧编码器集中视觉容量(454M参数),查询侧保持轻量(70M参数),匹配VDR的文本查询-图像文档输入不对称性。3) 发布两个变体,共享相同编码器和训练过程,仅在文档编码器的视觉tile预算上不同:DistilVDR-HiRes使用较大视觉token预算,DistilVDR-Fast使用3倍更小的视觉token预算。

Result: DistilVDR-HiRes在ViDoRe v1+v2+v3上达到61.74平均NDCG@5,达到8B教师模型性能的86.9%,并在高分辨率敏感的v3基准上领先所有复现的sub-1B基线。DistilVDR-Fast以3倍更小的视觉token预算达到59.98。两个变体存储100万文档的索引比最强sub-1B多向量基线小15.6倍,索引语料库速度提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: DistilVDR证明了从大规模教师模型到紧凑端到端单向量检索器的有效蒸馏路径,在保持接近教师模型性能的同时,大幅降低了索引存储和速度成本,为视觉文档检索提供了更实用的规模化方案。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval (VDR) is dominated by multi-billion-parameter models that are slow to index at full corpus scale and expensive to serve. Prior compression routes either train a smaller multi-vector encoder from scratch or distil only the query side; neither yields a compact single-vector retriever end-to-end. We present DistilVDR, a 524M end-to-end VDR system distilled bilaterally from a single 8B vision-language teacher under a pointwise cosine alignment loss. All supervision comes from the frozen teacher's embedding space, which was itself trained with relevance supervision, so the student objective needs no relevance labels, negative sampling, or contrastive term. We match VDR's text-query and image-document input asymmetry with an asymmetric encoder-only student that concentrates visual capacity on the document side and keeps the query side at 70M parameters. We release two variants that share the same encoders and training and differ only in the document encoder's visual-tile budget: DistilVDR-HiRes attains 61.74 average NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 (86.9% of the 8B teacher) and leads every reproduced sub-1B baseline on the high-resolution-sensitive v3 benchmark, while DistilVDR-Fast attains 59.98 at a 3 times smaller visual-token budget. Both variants store one million documents in a 15.6 times smaller index than the strongest sub-1B multi-vector baseline and index the corpus an order of magnitude faster. The code is available at https://github.com/Ryenhails/NanoVDR.

</details>


### [188] [ENTLORE: A Graph-Grounded Benchmark for Latent Organizational Reasoning in Enterprise Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10679)
*Akrin Zheng,Alexander Wu,Alaia Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ENTLORE，一个图基底的基准构建框架，用于评测企业问答中的"潜在组织推理"能力——即从日常文档中恢复语料库中不存在的隐含目标关系。基准包含2,341篇文档和907个问题，跨越显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理三类任务，实验表明即使提供黄金文档，潜在问题仍有30.4%无法回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业问答基准虽然提供真实的多源证据，但往往预先物化了答案路径，测试的是陈述事实的组合而非从语料中恢复不存在的目标关系（即潜在组织推理）。这是现有基准未能覆盖的关键能力缺口。

Method: ENTLORE框架从日常文档、权威组织表和操作记录中重建一个经过审计的企业世界。通过版本化的组织约定在真值图中认证派生关系，提供完整黄金答案和证明证书。对齐的匿名化发布仅暴露文档语料，隐藏私有结构和目标关系，诱导实体图或可导航知识库作为可部署的检索结构。

Result: 基准包含2,341篇文档（三种来源）和907个问题，在56种模型与访问配置下评估。结构化世界（诱导实体图或知识库）给出最强可部署结果。但即使提供黄金文档，潜在问题仍有30.4%无法回答，远高于显式问题（12.6%）和组合问题（6.2%）的未答率。

Conclusion: 企业问答不仅依赖文档召回，还取决于隐含组织关系是否变得可用。现有方法在潜在组织推理上存在显著差距，ENTLORE基准为这一能力提供了系统的评测框架和公开资源。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering is framed as retrieving internal documents and generating grounded answers. Routine enterprise records, however, are work by-products in which required organizational relations remain implicit across heterogeneous sources. Existing benchmarks provide realistic multi-source evidence, but often materialize a predefined answer path and therefore test the composition of stated facts rather than recovery of a target relation absent from the corpus. We call the latter capability latent organizational reasoning.
  We introduce ENTLORE, a graph-grounded benchmark construction framework that reconstructs an audited enterprise world from routine documents, authoritative organizational tables, and operational records. Versioned organizational conventions certify derived relations in a truth graph, enabling complete golden answers and proof certificates. The aligned anonymized release exposes only the document corpus while withholding private structure and target relations. ENTLORE contains 2,341 documents from three source types and 907 questions spanning explicit lookup, cross-source composition, and latent organizational reasoning, evaluated across 56 model and access configurations. Structuring the released world as an induced entity graph or navigable knowledge base gives the strongest deployable results. Yet supplying gold documents still leaves 30.4% of latent questions unanswered, versus 12.6% and 6.2% for explicit and compositional questions. Enterprise QA therefore depends not only on document recall, but also on whether implicit organizational relations become usable. The benchmark, data, and code are publicly available at ENTLORE.

</details>


### [189] [Deciding When to Rely on Visual Information: Gated Multimodal Fusion in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10700)
*Natalija Glisovic,Danica Kragic,Martin Tegner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VisGate框架，通过自适应门控机制按物品和用户序列上下文动态决定视觉信号的使用权重，并利用对比学习保持视觉与协同信号的互补性，而非强制对齐到共享空间。实验表明视觉效用随物品变化、在交互稀疏时更关键，且与视觉独特性语义相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐系统将视觉特征视为对所有物品和用户同等重要的通用信号，采用统一融合方式。但作者认为视觉效用是一个潜在的上下文变量，取决于物品本身和用户的交互历史，而非固定的物品属性。这种忽视视觉效用异质性的做法限制了推荐质量的提升。

Method: 提出VisGate框架：(1) 通过门控机制基于物品嵌入和用户当前序列上下文做出自适应的物品级融合决策；(2) 视觉表示通过序列共现模式的对比学习目标学习，保持与协同嵌入的互补性而非对齐到共享空间；(3) 学到的门控还可作为测量工具，用于理解视觉信息何时及为何有效。

Result: VisGate在推荐性能上具有竞争力；分析发现视觉效用在不同物品间存在差异，在交互稀疏（协同信号弱）时视觉信息贡献更大，且视觉效用与视觉独特性存在语义意义上的相关性。

Conclusion: 精细粒度的融合决策和模态互补性对多模态推荐都很重要；物品级视觉效用可以通过学习到的门控行为进行估计和解释，本文为理解多模态推荐中视觉信号的作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommender systems commonly fuse visual and collaborative signals uniformly, treating visual features as generically informative regardless of item or user context. We argue that visual utility, defined as the contribution of visual signals to recommendation quality, is a latent contextual variable that depends on both the item and the user's interaction history rather than a fixed item property. To model this variability, we introduce VisGate, a framework that makes adaptive item-level fusion decisions conditioned on item embeddings and the user's current sequence context. Visual representations are learned through a contrastive objective over sequential co-occurrence patterns, preserving complementarity with collaborative embeddings rather than aligning them into a shared space. Beyond achieving competitive recommendation performance, VisGate's learned gate serves as a measurement tool for understanding when and why visual information is beneficial. Our analyses show that visual utility varies across items, increases under interaction sparsity when collaborative signals are weak, and correlates with visual distinctiveness in semantically meaningful ways. Together, these findings highlight the importance of both fine-grained fusion and modality complementarity, while demonstrating that item-level visual utility can be estimated and interpreted through learned gating behaviour.

</details>


### [190] [TimeRoute: Time-Aware Modality Routing and Diffusion for Multi-Modal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10983)
*Pengyu Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Klim Zaporojets,Congfeng Cao,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeRoute，一个统一的基于扩散的推荐系统，通过时间感知的模态路由器为每个用户分配个性化的模态分布，并用基于FiLM的双流长短期去噪头对图重构器进行条件化，解决多模态推荐中模态时间尺度不匹配问题。在TikTok、Amazon-Baby和Amazon-Sports上相较强基线最多提升9.8%的Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐器融合协作信号与文本、图像、音频等物品模态，但不同模态的有用性随时间以不同速率漂移（如情人节时巧克力推荐从文本成分线索转向视觉包装和音频），导致两个耦合挑战：(1)不同时间上下文下用户需要不同的模态比例；(2)不太相关的模态更容易引入过时或误导性信号。现有工作使用全局共享的融合权重，无法处理这种动态性。

Method: TimeRoute包含两个核心组件：(1)时间感知的模态路由器，将每个用户的聚合行为特征映射为个性化的模态分布，替代先前工作使用的全局共享融合权重；(2)基于扩散的图重构器，通过FiLM（特征级线性调制）以相同的时间画像为条件，并配备双流长短期去噪头，在下游传播图形成前抑制过时的模态边。

Result: 在TikTok、Amazon-Baby和Amazon-Sports三个数据集上进行10折配对检验，TimeRoute在Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K上相较强基线取得了最高9.8%的一致改进。

Conclusion: TimeRoute通过将个性化时间感知的模态路由与扩散式图去噪相结合，有效解决了多模态推荐中的模态时间尺度不匹配问题，证明了在统一的扩散框架下联合处理模态分配与噪声抑制的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-modal recommenders fuse collaborative signals with item modalities such as text, images, and audio, but the usefulness of each drifts over time and at different rates. For example, chocolate purchases typically guided by textual ingredient cues can shift toward visual packaging and ambient audio around Valentine's Day. This modality time-scale mismatch gives rise to two coupled challenges: (1) users require different modality proportions across temporal contexts, and (2) less relevant modalities are more likely to introduce outdated or misleading signals into the recommender. We address both challenges within a unified diffusion-based recommender, TimeRoute. A temporal-aware modal router maps each user's aggregated behavioral features to a personalized modality distribution, replacing the globally shared fusion weights used in prior work. The diffusion-based graph reconstructor is then conditioned on the same temporal profile through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) with dual-stream long- and short-term denoising heads, suppressing outdated modality edges before they enter the propagation graph. Experiments on TikTok, Amazon-Baby, and Amazon-Sports demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 9.8\% in Recall@K, Precision@K, and NDCG@K over strong baselines across 10-seed paired tests. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TimeRoute.

</details>


### [191] [Sona Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11015)
*Sona Team,Alexandr Udeneev,Aleksei Krasilnikov,Alexey Nadtochiy,Andrey Semenov,Andrey Tsyrkunov,Anna Krivonos,Anna Lipkina,Artem Matveev,Daniil Burlakov,Daniil Leschev,Daria Tikhonovich,Denis Burshtein,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Eugene Krofto,Grigorii Khlystov,Ilya Murzin,Kirill Golovko,Ksenia Sycheva,Leonid Dmitriev,Mariia Rozaeva,Mariia Ulianova,Mikhail Sandul,Nikolai Savushkin,Oleg Sorokin,Roman Odobesku,Semyon Panenko,Sergei Liamaev,Sergei Makeev,Vadim Shilov,Veronika Ivanova,Viktor Yanush,Vladimir Baikalov,Vladislav Dodonov,Vladislav Tytskiy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Sona是Yandex Music推出的单一生成式推荐模型，用一个联合训练的单模型替换了包含15+候选生成器及预排序/排序模块的完整生产级联系统，在在线A/B测试中显著提升了关键参与度指标（活跃用户+4.53%、总收听时长+6.30%、点赞+11.42%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产推荐系统采用复杂的多级联架构（15+候选生成器+预排序/排序模型），依赖数百个手工特征和大型transformer信号，系统庞大且维护成本高。作者希望验证一个单一联合训练的生成式模型能否替代成熟的多阶段级联系统，同时提升推荐质量。

Method: Sona架构将候选生成和排序统一在共享的用户表示上。编码器将用户时间序列的日志事件序列转化为隐藏状态，供自回归解码器和排序模块共同使用。通过下一词预测和蒸馏目标联合更新编码器，使生成与排序通过同一用户状态耦合。训练时使用更大的教师模型提供排序目标，但推理时仅部署单一模型（编码器+解码器+排序模块），不使用任何手工特征。

Result: 在Yandex Music最大推荐场景之一（智能音箱上的My Vibe）在线A/B实验中，相比生产对照组，Sona在活跃用户数上提升4.53%（主要指标），总收听时长提升6.30%，点赞数提升11.42%。活跃用户提升是此前最强模型Argus增量收益的2.35倍。

Conclusion: 实验表明，一个联合训练的单一模型可以替代成熟的多阶段推荐级联系统，同时在实际流量上提升推荐质量。这验证了生成式单一模型架构在工业级推荐系统中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: We introduce Sona, a single-model generative recommender for Yandex Music. In an online A/B test, Sona replaced the entire production cascade, comprising more than 15 candidate generators followed by pre-ranking and ranking models that consume hundreds of features, including signals from large transformer models such as Argus and target-attention scorers, while significantly improving key engagement metrics.
  The architecture of Sona unifies candidate generation and ranking around a shared user representation. Its encoder transforms the user's chronological sequence of logged engagement events into hidden states consumed by both the autoregressive decoder and the Ranking Module. The next-token-prediction and distillation objectives jointly update the encoder, coupling generation and ranking through the same user state. Neither Sona nor its Teacher Ranker uses hand-engineered features; both operate on logged event fields and learned item representations. In the final Sona configuration, the larger teacher supplies ranking targets during training but is absent from serving, leaving the encoder, decoder, and Ranking Module as a single deployed model.
  We evaluate Sona in an online A/B experiment using live traffic from My Vibe on smart speakers, one of Yandex Music's largest recommendation surfaces. Relative to the production control, Sona produced statistically significant uplifts of 4.53% in Active Users, the primary metric, 6.30% in Total Listening Time, and 11.42% in Likes. These effects were incremental to improvements retained from preceding deployments. The Active Users uplift was 2.35 times the increment previously delivered by Argus, the strongest model deployed on this surface before Sona. These results show that a single jointly trained model can replace a mature multi-stage recommendation cascade while improving recommendation quality on live traffic.

</details>


### [192] [Self-Knowledge Retrieval Augmented Generation Framework for Patent Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11030)
*Jian Zhang,Songlin Lei,Zhuohao Yang,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自知识检索增强生成（Self-Knowledge RAG）的专利检索与匹配框架，引导大语言模型自主提取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，结合FAISS检索与生成式匹配机制，显著提升专利检索与匹配的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专利匹配方法存在两大局限：1）基于领域预训练或指令微调的方法需要大量人工标注且存在灾难性遗忘问题；2）传统RAG方法引入外部知识但未能充分发挥LLM自动解析专利和挖掘深层语义关系的能力。专利文档结构复杂、术语密集且包含多模态信息，传统方法难以准确识别专利间的细微差异。

Method: 提出自知识RAG框架：1）引导LLM从专利匹配查询中自主提取关键技术实体；2）构建层级本体结构以实现查询扩展；3）集成FAISS向量检索与生成式匹配机制；4）利用自知识增强模型对专利创新的理解能力。

Result: 在真实专利数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法展现出卓越性能，验证了其有效性与应用潜力。

Conclusion: 自知识RAG框架通过让LLM自主解析专利并构建结构化知识，克服了传统微调方法的高成本问题和传统RAG的外部知识利用不足问题，为专利检索与匹配提供了一种更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Patent retrieval and matching based on large language models (LLMs) play a vital role in intellectual property protection. However, due to the complex structure of patent documents, dense technical terminology, and multi-modal information, traditional methods struggle to accurately identify subtle differences between patents. Existing LLM-based patent matching approaches typically rely on domain-specific pretrained or instruction tuning, which often entail high manual labeling costs and catastrophic forgetting. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods introduce external knowledge they fail to fully leverage LLM's capability to automatically parse patents and mine deep semantic relationships. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a self-knowledge RAG framework that guides LLMs to autonomously extract key technical entities and construct hierarchical ontological structures from patent matching queries, thereby enabling query expansion and precise retrieval. The method integrates the FAISS retrieval with a generative matching mechanism, leveraging self-knowledge to enhance the model's understanding of patent innovations and significantly improve retrieval and matching accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate the outstanding performance of the proposed method on real-world patent datasets, validating its effectiveness and application potential.

</details>


### [193] [Role of Personality in Conversational Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11164)
*Abdisalam Abukar,Junchen Fu,Chengli Zhai,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验（26名参与者、3种助手人格×3类任务）考察了大语言模型助手人格对对话式信息搜寻行为的影响。结果显示助手人格具有行为区分度（外向型产出更长轮次、尽责型引发更高用户词占比和更多轮次），最强的效应是任务与助手的交互作用对信任和委托的影响，不同任务偏好不同的人格风格，但参与者强烈偏好风格选择或自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地用于对话式信息搜寻，在其中的助手不仅检索和生成内容，还塑造用户如何阐述约束、追问、验证声明和判断答案是否足够，但用户人格、助手人格和任务情境如何共同影响这些交互尚不清楚，因此需要将人格作为可控变量进行研究。

Method: 采用受控组内设计（within-subject）：实验操纵助手人格（外向、尽责、中性三种条件）和任务类型（探索性旅行规划、对比性手机购物、验证敏感的健康饮食信息），用Big Five量表测量参与者人格。26名参与者每人完成3个任务×3种助手人格条件，收集对话日志、行为痕迹、交互后问卷、退出问卷和Big Five数据。

Result: 助手条件在行为上显著不同：外向型助手产生更长轮次，尽责型助手引发更高用户词占比和更多轮次，中性基线介于两者之间。最强的效应是任务与助手的交互作用对信任和委托的影响，不同任务偏好的风格各异。没有出现全局最优助手，但参与者强烈偏好风格选择或自适应。

Conclusion: 助手人格应被视为一种情境敏感的交互设计变量，而非可全局优化的系统属性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for information seeking, where users find, compare, and evaluate information through dialogue. In this role, the assistant does more than retrieve or generate content: it shapes how users articulate constraints, ask follow-up questions, verify claims, and decide when an answer is sufficient for action. Yet little is known about how user personality, assistant personality, and task context jointly influence these interactions. We examine personality as a controllable variable in conversational information seeking and study its effects on user behaviour and interaction quality. We conducted a controlled within-subject study in which assistant personality and task type were experimentally varied, while participant personality was measured using Big Five scores. Twenty-six participants each completed three information-seeking tasks under three assistant personality conditions: extraverted, conscientious, and neutral. Tasks covered exploratory travel planning, comparative smartphone shopping, and verification-sensitive health and diet information seeking. Data included conversation logs, behavioural traces, post-interaction questionnaires, an exit questionnaire, and Big Five measures. The assistant conditions were behaviourally distinct: the extraverted assistant produced longer turns, the conscientious assistant elicited higher user word share and more turns, and the neutral baseline fell between them. The strongest effect was a task-by-assistant interaction on trust and delegation, with preferred styles varying by task. No global winner emerged, but participants strongly preferred style choice or adaptation. These findings position assistant personality as a context-sensitive interactional design variable rather than a globally optimisable system property.

</details>


### [194] [Are We Really Making Progress in Group Recommendation? Unmasking the Tie-Breaking Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11190)
*Song-Duo Ma,Pu-Jen Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示群体推荐方法中存在的系统性评估偏差：训练时的分数压缩（额外的sigmoid变换）与评估时的确定性平局打破相互作用，导致HR@K和NDCG@K等top-K指标被高估。作者提出tie-aware评估协议（在均匀随机平局打破下精确计算指标期望），并在CAMRa2011和Mafengwo数据集上重新评估多个近期方法，发现许多改进在tie-aware评估下大幅缩水，方法排名也会显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期群体推荐方法在标准基准上报告了强劲改进，但作者质疑这些提升是否真正反映了群体偏好建模的实质性进展，怀疑存在系统性评估偏差。

Method: 1) 识别偏差来源：BPR目标函数前添加sigmoid变换导致训练时分数压缩，配合评估时的确定性平局打破产生高估。2) 提出tie-aware评估协议，在均匀随机平局打破假设下计算HR@K和NDCG@K的精确期望。3) 在CAMRa2011和Mafengwo数据集上，于群体和用户推荐两种设置下重新评估近期代表性方法及其基线。4) 分析额外的sigmoid是否充当隐式边距平滑，并探索温度缩放的BPR作为替代方案。

Result: 许多先前报告的改进在tie-aware评估下显著缩水，方法的相对排名会发生明显变化。额外sigmoid可能充当优化过程中的隐式边距平滑，而温度缩放的BPR能在不引发严重平局膨胀的情况下保留大部分收益。

Conclusion: tie-aware评估对于在群体推荐中建立可靠的进展至关重要，研究结果强调社区需要采用更严谨的评估协议来衡量方法改进。

Abstract: Recent group recommendation methods have reported strong improvements on standard benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains always reflect genuine advances in modeling group preferences. In this paper, we show that several recent methods are affected by a systematic evaluation bias caused by the interaction between training-time score compression and evaluation-time deterministic tie-breaking. Specifically, an additional sigmoid transformation before the BPR objective can greatly increase tied top scores, making top-K metrics such as HR@K and NDCG@K highly sensitive to how ties are resolved. We revisit recent representative methods and their baselines on CAMRa2011 and Mafengwo under both group and user recommendation settings, and evaluate them with a tie-aware protocol that computes the exact expectation of HR@K and NDCG@K under uniform random tie-breaking. Our results show that many previously reported improvements shrink substantially under tie-aware evaluation, and the relative ranking of methods can change markedly. We further show that the additional sigmoid may act as implicit margin smoothing during optimization, and that temperature-scaled BPR can retain much of this benefit without inducing severe tie inflation. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of tie-aware evaluation for establishing reliable progress in group recommendation. The code is available at https://github.com/songduoma/TieAwareGroupRec.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoX Genesis框架，以持久化软件项目为核心而非持久化智能体，让有限生命周期的智能体在递归世界中协作开发长期软件项目。通过构建Rust语言C编译器（250k行，120+小时，仅44美元）和重实现MESA模块（1.55-6.87x加速比）等实验，展示了持久项目组织的可行性和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统的开发周期远超任何单个编码智能体的生命周期。现有智能体系统通过持久会话、记忆、管理器或共享上下文来维持连续性，但这种方式与智能体本身绑定。作者提出质疑：能否将注意力从持久化智能体转向持久化软件项目本身，让所有智能体有限存活？

Method: Genesis将软件表示为持久递归世界：每个局部世界由已接受的版本和仓库路径定位；有限存活的智能体提出局部变更；递归委托在路径间移动工作；只有被接受的后果才推进持久版本历史。这种组织方式覆盖了软件开发的三个关键阶段：形成（formation）、延续（continuation）和再开发（redevelopment）。

Result: 1) 使用DeepSeek V4 Flash构建了约250k行的Rust C编译器，运行超120小时，归档超1000个智能体片段，仅花费44美元token费用，通过完整c-testsuite及大部分LLVM和Csmith测试；2) 使用GLM 5.2生成的编译器世界在反复替换智能体后保持完整测试性能；3) 重实现了13个MESA模块（100k+ Fortran行转为近90k Rust行），六个数值负载取得1.55-6.87x中位加速比。

Conclusion: 结果表明，长周期软件开发可以围绕持久项目而非持久智能体进行组织。这种范式解耦了软件项目的生命周期与智能体的生命周期，为超长周期自主软件开发提供了新思路。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [196] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N2NMatcher，一个面向内联（inlining）鲁棒的程序级二进制代码相似性分析（BCSA）框架，通过预测稳定锚点函数进行模块分解和匹配，提升在函数内联场景下的分析效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序级BCSA方法通常基于函数调用图（FCG）结构分解二进制程序并匹配模块，但函数内联会改变FCG结构和二进制函数语义，导致现有分解和模块匹配方法效果下降。

Method: 首先进行实证研究验证跨编译设置下是否存在稳定模块边界的锚点函数；然后使用层次图神经网络（编码ACFG-FCG表示，包含操作码序列、控制流结构和FCG调用上下文）预测锚点函数；最后执行锚点限定的模块分解，并使用学习得到的模块图嵌入进行模块匹配。

Result: 实验结果表明N2NMatcher在分解质量和模块匹配准确率上均优于现有方法，能够实现更有效的程序级BCSA。

Conclusion: 通过预测稳定锚点函数来进行模块分解与匹配，N2NMatcher有效提升了函数内联场景下程序级二进制代码相似性分析的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [197] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过函数级测量跟踪Linux IIO子系统中10,117个补丁序列的函数轨迹，发现代码审查对补丁的修改大多是表面的——75.3%的轨迹从未被文本修改，而真正被编辑的函数语义相似度极高（0.990 vs 0.909基线）。修改主要集中在第一轮审查，且这种表观集中主要是统计假象，控制后仅有微小的残余效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于代码审查对补丁影响的研究通常只分析最终合并的补丁，而缺乏与首次提交版本的比较，无法准确测量审查过程对代码的实际修改程度。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过函数级轨迹追踪来量化审查期间代码的真实变化。

Method: 追踪Linux IIO子系统补丁历史中10,117个函数轨迹（每个函数跨补丁系列的编号修订版本跟踪），计算相似度分数并与无关函数对作为基线比较。通过区分文本修改与未修改轨迹、按审查轮次分析，并控制统计偏差来揭示真实修改模式。

Result: 朴素统计显示近乎完全相似，但这是组合假象：75.3%的轨迹从未被修改（100%相似度抬高了平均值）。仅看实际编辑的轨迹，语义保留度仍很高（0.990 vs 0.909基线）。修改集中出现在第一轮审查，但这主要是因为后续轮次有更多未触及的函数，而非编辑变得更保守。控制后仍存在统计显著但微小的残余效应。

Conclusion: 研究提出一个开放性问题：接近满分的相似度究竟反映的是函数语义的真实保留，还是测量工具无法捕捉小而局部编辑的重要意义。本文作为初步探究，指出了下一步研究方向。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [198] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了自进化（self-evolving）harness在编程智能体中的表现，通过在八种编程语言（Multi-SWE-Bench）和三种基础模型上固定进化策略，解耦了"基准特定适应""语言工程知识"和"模型缺陷补偿"三类因素。研究发现：进化harness通过可证伪的契约记录每一次修改，能补偿模型的可恢复执行缺陷（缩小"能做"与"实际做"之间的差距）；跨语言共享抽象剧本但生态系统机制几乎不相交；共享核心可迁移并蒸馏为通用harness，而生态系统边际则拒绝迁移、需要原生重新进化。结论将进化harness重新定义为"可读的补偿层"，由语言工程需求与模型行为缺口共同塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于自进化harness的研究只报告聚合收益，未分析进化产物究竟编码了什么——是基准特定适应、语言特定工程知识，还是对基础模型局限性的补偿？作者希望解耦这三类因素，理解进化harness的本质。

Method: 将进化策略固定，在8种编程语言（Multi-SWE-Bench）×3种基础模型的网格上运行自我进化闭环；每次编辑都通过类型化失败信号路由，并记录为可证伪的契约，使每个修改在进化后都可归因；通过对比最小种子、mini-SWE-agent脚手架和进化harness的表现进行分析。

Result: (1)进化循环在多数单元中提升留出集解决率，但存在两个零收益区域；(2)增益是对可恢复执行缺陷的补偿，缺陷质量与增益近零相关，且主导缺陷具有单元特异性——harness弥合了策略"能做"与"实际做"之间的差距；(3)各语言进化harness共享抽象剧本，但用几乎不相交的语言生态系统机制实例化；(4)共享核心可迁移并被蒸馏为通用harness，但生态系统边际拒绝迁移、需原生重新进化。

Conclusion: 自进化harness应被理解为"可读的补偿层"，由语言的工程需求和模型的behavior缺口共同塑造，而非不透明的基准调优脚手架。这为理解进化系统的内部机制和迁移性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [199] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性案例研究，在组织内的四个AI项目中识别出六种"偏见气味"（bias smells），作为公平性债务的早期预警指标，涵盖技术与人员两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性债务源于AI开发缺陷可能带来的社会危害，但现有关于从业者如何在开发过程中识别公平性债务潜在来源的证据十分有限，亟需实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 对某组织内四个AI项目进行探索性案例研究，通过半结构化访谈和开放式问卷收集25名专业人士的数据，辅以内部沟通渠道和项目文档的观察，采用迭代定性编码、备忘录和持续比较法进行分析。

Result: 识别出六种反复出现的指标（偏见气味）：上下文过度简化、数据集不平衡、指标不充分、临时测试、个体多样性忽视、同质化团队构成。这些气味横跨软件开发的技术与人员层面，标志着可能引入或强化偏见并导致公平性债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏见气味将软件气味范式扩展至公平性领域，为通过可观察指标将公平性纳入软件质量保障提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [200] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于符号执行的以太坊智能合约分析工具Mythril产生的误报问题，通过分析误报的根本原因，设计了一种基于规则的方案来减少误报，在保持真实漏洞检测能力的同时显著降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约安全分析工具（如基于符号执行的工具）虽然能检测漏洞，但会产生大量误报，耗费安全研究人员大量时间和精力去排查。因此需要提高工具可靠性，减少误报率。

Method: 首先对Mythril产生的漏洞报告进行系统性分析，确定了误报产生的根本原因；基于这些洞察设计规则化（rule-based）方法来过滤误报，并为Mythril中影响最大的漏洞类型实现了相应规则。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在不影响真实漏洞检测能力的前提下，显著减少了误报数量，提升了Mythril工具的整体可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过分析误报根因并结合规则化过滤，可以有效提升基于符号执行的智能合约分析工具的准确性，使其更适合实际安全审计场景。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [201] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia是一个Eclipse插件，将结构化依赖可视化与LLM代码解释集成在同一交互式图上，用于Java程序理解。它通过多边类型依赖图、基于图感知Callee剪枝（GACP）的LLM解释、克隆检测覆盖层和CVE风险覆盖层四大支柱，帮助开发者理解代码的同时保持控制权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的程序理解工具要么仅提供静态依赖图（缺乏语义解释），要么仅依赖LLM生成解释（缺乏结构上下文且不可追溯）。作者希望通过结合结构化依赖可视化与LLM解释，在共享的交互式图谱上实现更可靠、可审计的Java程序理解。

Method: Comprendia包含四大支柱：（1）多边类型依赖图，支持实时搜索和多种布局；（2）基于GACP的LLM解释，GACP利用图距离、继承折叠和边类型权重来选择相关callee生成可复现且可追溯的提示；（3）克隆检测覆盖层，高亮重复代码并建议提取到父类的重构机会；（4）基于OSV.dev的CVE风险覆盖层。

Result: 作者在包含已知克隆和漏洞的Java项目上演示了Comprendia，展示了统一图谱基底如何支持程序理解，同时让开发者保持控制权。

Conclusion: Comprendia证明了将依赖图可视化和LLM解释集成在同一交互介质上是可行的，GACP策略能够产生跨LLM家族可复现且可追溯到图谱节点的提示，为程序理解工具提供了更透明、可审计的范式。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [202] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项前瞻性、预注册的随机实验，检验了大型语言模型在将理论翻译为可执行程序时，不同的呈现格式（结构化干预契约 vs 连贯散文）是否系统性改变其输出。结果显示，两种假设（H1和H2）均未获得支持，相同理论的可辨识性接近随机水平，说明渲染格式并未产生预期的统一、家族不变、可分类的行为几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口头/文字理论与可执行程序之间不存在直接映射——翻译过程需要对变量、干预和交互做大量选择。研究者希望系统性地考察：当理论内容相同但呈现格式不同时，LLM生成的程序是否会发生系统性变化，从而界定格式效应在LLM理论到程序翻译中的边界。

Method: 采用前瞻性预注册随机实验：16个独立分配位将32个配对渲染器槽分配给结构化干预契约和连贯散文两种格式；两个固定的LLM快照翻译5个匿名理论描述，产生320个预先授权的单次程序（使用冻结的稀疏二次语言）；确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；两个主要终点评估跨模型匹配距离缩减和封闭集同理论可辨识性，结合精确随机化推断和27项预注册支持标准，在符号线性和量级排序两条管道上执行。

Result: H1和H2均返回注册裁决"不支持"；108项标准评估中仅19项通过。同理论可辨识性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523，注册阈值为0.80）。仅有一个H2匹配距离终点在符号线性管道中通过多重性校正，但其对应的量级排序结果未达到注册效应量下限，不满足联合支持规则。

Conclusion: 渲染格式并未产生预先预测的统一、家族不变、可分类的行为几何。这一结果对LLM理论到程序翻译中的规范格式效应设定了具体边界，同时提供了完全可审计的随机化设计、数据集和软件，供可执行形式化研究使用。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [203] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从开发者与LLM智能体的交互轨迹中提取可复用开发者偏好的框架，包含基于规则的引导生成、证据驱动的细化以及可复现的回放评估。在206个真实会话实验中发现，个性化技能相比无技能基线提升微弱且不稳定，而跨开发者池化的通用技能效果最佳，表明广泛的程序性知识比开发者特定偏好更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者与编码智能体的长期协作中会涌现出个人偏好，捕捉并复用这些偏好可以减少重复纠正、改善协作效率。现有工作主要聚焦于任务特定技能，缺乏对从交互历史中提炼的开发者特定技能能否泛化到未来任务的验证。

Method: 提出一个提取可复用开发者偏好的框架：(1) 通过基于规则的引导机制生成个性化技能；(2) 通过证据驱动的细化过程完善技能；(3) 使用可复现的重放框架，配合交互式、轨迹条件化的LLM模拟开发者进行评估。实验涵盖13位开发者的206个真实会话，对比无技能、通用技能及其他用户技能等基线。

Result: 个性化技能相比无技能基线仅带来微小且不稳定的改进，而跨开发者池化的通用技能实现了最大且最一致的提升。进一步分析表明，当开发者偏好出现频率较高、且历史中包含与未来任务相关的多个示例时，个性化技能效果更好。

Conclusion: 研究为"开发者特定个性化何时有效"提供了实证洞察，表明广泛可迁移的程序性知识比开发者特定的偏好信号更具鲁棒性。这挑战了单纯追求个性化的直觉，提示在智能体技能设计中应重视跨开发者通用知识的价值。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [204] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CausalRepair 是一个基于最小因果上下文的会话式自动程序修复框架，通过静态切片和动态切片双重策略构建紧凑、因果相关的修复上下文，在Defects4J数据集上修复了313个bug，平均修复成本仅0.029美元，优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的APR方法存在因果差距：测试上下文可能嘈杂或不完整，静态分析得到的源代码上下文常包含无关和未执行的代码，误导LLM难以定位真正的根因。

Method: 提出CausalRepair框架，采用双切片策略：基于上下文的静态切片净化测试语义，基于执行轨迹的动态切片捕获源代码中精确的运行时依赖关系，两者共同构建紧凑且因果相关的上下文来指导迭代修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0和Defects4J-Trans上使用DeepSeek-V3评估，正确修复了313个bug，优于ReinFix和TSAPR等最先进方法，平均修复成本降至0.029美元/bug。

Conclusion: 通过构建最小因果上下文而非噪声或完整的上下文，CausalRepair有效缩小了LLM在APR中的因果差距，显著提升了修复效果并大幅降低了修复成本。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3.
