CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [205] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Cursor编辑器中配置文件提示词（.cursorrules文件）进行了大规模的实证研究，分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个配置文件，揭示了其分布、演化和维护特征，并开发了一个包含65个代码的分类体系来捕捉开发者如何表达编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI辅助编程已广泛普及，提示工程受到越来越多的关注，但以往研究主要聚焦于对话式提示词，而配置文件提示词（如.cursorrules）尚未得到充分研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，理解配置文件提示词的实际使用情况。

Method: 作者收集并分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个.cursorrules文件，进行定量分析以刻画其分布、演化和维护特征；同时随机抽取65个提示文件进行定性分析，开发了一个包含65个代码的分类体系（codebook），涵盖编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑等方面。

Result: 研究发现.cursorrules文件从2024年中期开始迅速涌现，采用集中在小型、低活跃度、单一维护者的仓库中，暗示其更多用于玩具项目而非专业开发。内容以代码质量和工程实践、项目结构和配置、可维护性为主，安全相关内容出现频率较低。同时发现.cursorrules与当前标准的.mdc文件在主题和话题上存在延续性。

Conclusion: 配置文件提示词在AI辅助编程中呈现快速增长的态势，但其使用主要集中在个人/小型项目中。提示内容侧重于工程实践和质量指导，安全方面的关注不足。从.cursorrules到.mdc的演进保持了主题的连续性，表明配置文件提示词正在标准化发展。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [206] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的多LLM集成框架，用于汽车HiL验证中的传感器级故障分类。该框架使用紧凑的证据表示，结合异构大语言模型的输出，通过置信度加权投票提升诊断鲁棒性。在汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统上，Top-3集成达到0.917 Top-1准确率、0.913宏F1和0.902 MCC，优于单一模型，且Top-5集成未超越Top-3，表明模型互补性比集成规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL验证产生大量多变量测试记录，传统分析方法依赖人工审查和基于规则的方法，效率低且难以处理复杂故障。现有机器学习/深度学习方法需要大量标注数据、跨工况泛化能力差，且缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种既能提升诊断准确率又能提供可解释决策的自动化故障分类方案。

Method: 提出可解释的多LLM集成框架：(1) 将故障注入记录转化为紧凑的证据表示；(2) 集成多个异构大语言模型（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B等）；(3) 使用置信度加权投票策略组合各模型输出；(4) 评估不同Top-K集成配置（Top-3 vs Top-5）的性能差异。在汽油发动机和电动汽车两个HiL系统、三种驾驶工况、十种单故障类别上进行评估。

Result: 单一模型中Mistral Small 24B表现最佳（Top-1准确率0.903，MCC 0.887，Brier分数0.102）。Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B + Qwen2.5 32B + Phi-4 14B）通过置信度加权投票将场景平均结果提升至Top-1准确率0.917、宏F1 0.913、MCC 0.902，并具有最佳校准性能。Top-5集成未超越Top-3，证明模型互补性比集成规模更关键。

Conclusion: 协调的多LLM推理能够为汽车HiL验证提供鲁棒、校准良好且工程师可解释的故障分类。模型之间的互补性对于集成性能至关重要，合理选择异构模型组合比单纯增加模型数量更有效。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [207] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PPProbe，一个专门针对概率Datalog分析中的冲突提取器。概率Datalog扩展允许静态分析按可能性排序警报，但边际概率不保证联合可满足性，导致输出中可能包含相互不一致但各自合理的警报。PPProbe将这种不一致形式化为最小不可满足子集(MUS)，利用Datalog推导图的结构引导搜索并剪枝，在70个基准上比最先进的MUS枚举器快2.5-24倍，平均过滤47.7%的相互不一致警报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog分析引入了确定性分析中不存在的新挑战：边际概率只保证单个警报的可信度，但不保证多个警报之间存在联合可满足性，导致开发者可能在不可能共存的警报组合上浪费精力。

Method: PPProbe利用Datalog推导图的结构特性来引导冲突搜索，通过自底向上的UNSAT推理来剪枝搜索空间，而非一般性地改进MUS枚举算法。

Result: 在70个基准（涵盖功耗侧信道分析、数据竞争检测、语义差异和贝叶斯网络推理）上，PPProbe的吞吐量比最先进的MUS枚举器高2.5-24倍，其识别的冲突可保守估计减少47.7%的误报。

Conclusion: 利用Datalog推导图结构的专用冲突提取器能有效解决概率Datalog分析中的警报不一致问题，显著提升分析效率并减少开发者的无效调查工作。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [208] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文继续为NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予基于三重图文法（TGGs）的形式语义，并针对重叠查询翻译这一缺口引入了一种TGG规则的skip语义，从而为模型与视图之间的自动化增量同步提供形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视图的开发是管理复杂系统之系统复杂性和促进协作的重要技术，这对视图定义语言提出了高要求：既要易于使用且通用，又要具备精确的形式语义以支持可控的视图集成和整体系统一致性。

Method: 延续先前工作，使用三重图文法（TGGs）为NeoJoin视图定义语言提供形式语义；通过引入skip语义（即允许规则根据应用上下文跳过某些规定的动作）来解决重叠查询翻译支持不足的缺口。

Result: 该方法为模型与视图之间获得具有高形式化保证的自动化增量同步铺平了道路，同时作为TGGs表达力和可用性的又一案例研究。

Conclusion: 引入的skip语义有效弥补了重叠查询翻译的缺口，进一步验证了TGGs在处理视图定义语言语义方面的能力。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs. We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [209] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个量子软件模式挖掘流水线qpa，该工具通过扩展匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，在80个开源项目中检测到全部23种量子模式，并在未见框架Qrisp上达到0.712的micro-F1分数（未经扩展时为0.449）。作者还构建了模式组合图来分析模式使用与共现情况，并开源了知识库、数据集及流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式提供了构建量子程序的高级抽象，但缺乏关于这些模式在实际项目中如何被采用的实证证据，需要量化研究和工具支持。

Method: 扩展已有量子模式目录至61种模式，构建链接框架组件与模式的知识库（覆盖5个来源的286个组件），开发模式挖掘工具，增加两个额外匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，并构建组合图存入图数据库以分析模式组装和共现关系。

Result: 在80个项目中检测到全部23种模式；在未见过的框架Qrisp上micro-F1达到0.712（无词汇扩展时为0.449）；产出包含286个框架组件的知识库和模式使用数据集。

Conclusion: 词汇扩展能显著提升模式检测的准确性，组合图可用于分析模式装配和共现机制，开源流水线与知识库支持可复现的量子模式研究。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [210] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GitSkills数据集，包含从282,200个公共GitHub仓库收集的3,797,117个SKILL.md文件（即agent技能文件），这些文件是2025年10月Anthropic推出的开放规范。数据集保留所有文件出现记录并分组为1,877,981个不同内容，以单个SQLite文件形式支持对agent技能的采纳、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能文件与SE社区通常挖掘的代码工件不同：它们主要用自然语言编写、由模型在运行时概率性选择、没有编译器或类型检查器验证选择，也没有中心注册表或包管理器，靠复制文件夹传播。因此需要实证研究来了解开发者如何编写、复用和维护这些技能，但目前没有数据集记录这一群体。

Method: 作者构建了GitSkills数据集：从282,200个公共仓库中收集3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，保留每次文件出现及其仓库、路径和内容哈希。将相同文件分组为1,877,981个不同内容，并为每组一个代表性文件补充全文、解析的front matter、文件夹内容、仓库元数据以及部分文件的提交历史。所有数据打包为单个自包含的SQLite文件。

Result: 产出了一个包含数百万技能文件的数据集，支持对agent技能在采纳、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全等维度上的研究，数据以单一自包含SQLite文件形式提供。

Conclusion: GitSkills填补了agent技能实证研究的数据空白，为研究社区提供了研究这一新兴软件开发范式的基础设施，涵盖技能的采纳、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全性等多个研究方向。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>


### [211] [Understanding the Architecture of Coding Agents: An Exploratory Study Using a Research Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10934)
*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地记录和描述了编码智能体（Coding Agents）的主要架构组件，并提出Ark——一个极简开源编码智能体用于研究和教学，以及ArkBench基准测试。使用gpt-5.4-mini，Ark在10个任务中解决了8个，并与现有SOTA编码智能体架构进行了对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已成为AI辅助软件开发的主要接口，但其内部架构鲜为人知，缺乏像编译器和操作系统那样系统化的架构描述文档。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 文档化编码智能体的核心架构组件、职责、交互和执行流程；2) 构建最小化开源编码智能体Ark，保留现代编码智能体的关键架构机制但强调简洁清晰；3) 引入ArkBench轻量级基准（10个代表性软件维护与演进任务）；4) 利用近期提出的架构分类法将Ark与SOTA编码智能体进行对比。

Result: 使用gpt-5.4-mini模型，Ark成功解决了10个任务中的8个，且token消耗适中；Ark的架构与现有SOTA编码智能体的对比通过分类法完成。

Conclusion: Ark和ArkBench为编码智能体的教学、研究和实验提供了实用基础，填补了该领域缺乏系统性架构描述的空白。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [212] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Configuration Management (ACM)，一个框架无关的配置治理参考模型，用于异构智能体系统的版本化配置管理和治理。通过语义投影将异构原生配置归一化为规范的配置图，并基于有限格上的单调传播形式化影响语义，实现可复现、可审计的治理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLMOps和AgentOps平台支持编排和可观测性，但缺乏统一的配置治理模型来将异构智能体系统表示为连贯且版本化的配置。随着智能体系统日益复杂（包含异构代理、提示词、工具、模型、策略和工作流），需要一个共同的配置治理模型来管理这些系统的生命周期和变更。

Method: ACM框架包含：1) 类型化和独立版本化的Agentic配置项；2) 不可变修订和基线；3) 显式配置-运行时分离；4) 生命周期和保障语义；5) 依赖感知的影响传播；6) 运行时溯源。异构原生配置通过语义投影归一化为规范配置图，并提供一个Python参考实现，支持LangGraph、CrewAI和OpenAI Agents SDK适配器。

Result: 通过27个治理场景和9个定量影响传播案例进行评估。三个框架在投影后产生治理等价的ACM表示和可复现的治理结果。影响语义被形式化为有限格上的单调传播，确立了收敛性、终止性和初始影响估值之上最小不动点的唯一性。

Conclusion: 实验证据表明，在评估范围内，通用治理语义可以支持跨异构智能体执行抽象的可复现性、可审计性、依赖分析和互操作性，为智能体系统的配置治理提供了统一的理论基础和实践参考实现。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [A Graph Neural Network--Guided Genetic Algorithm for Physical Internet Supply Chain Optimization under Cost Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10245)
*Faezeh Ardali,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理互联网（Physical Internet）网络中工厂-枢纽分配、工厂供应、横向转运、零售商配送和短缺的库存与分销联合规划问题。作者提出一个三层网络（工厂、枢纽、零售商）的确定性与min-max后悔模型，并开发了图神经网络引导的遗传算法（GNN-GA）来解决分配决策。GNN用于估计枢纽特定的工厂选择概率以构建初始种群和自适应变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理互联网网络中的库存与分销规划需要同时协调工厂-枢纽分配、供应、协作枢纽间的横向转运、零售商配送及短缺等决策，问题同时包含离散分配决策和相互依赖的连续流，且不确定的运行成本使鲁棒规划更加困难。传统方法难以高效求解这一组合优化问题的复杂性。

Method: 1) 构建三层网络（工厂、枢纽、零售商）的确定性模型和min-max后悔鲁棒模型。2) 开发GNN-GA算法：GNN估计枢纽特定的工厂选择概率，用于构造GA初始种群，并根据预测不确定性自适应调整变异。3) 对每个未见过的候选分配方案，通过求解剩余连续流问题至LP最优来评估。4) 与模拟退火和标准GA在15个实例上进行对比，使用匹配随机种子和固定评估预算。

Result: 在15个测试实例上对比了模拟退火、标准GA和GNN-GA。在精确可解的实例13上进行了400次评估实验（允许三代完整后代和部分第四代），GNN-GA在全部10次匹配运行中均优于GA。消融实验表明学习的初始化贡献了大部分改进，而熵引导的变异效果较小且依赖实例。

Conclusion: GNN-GA通过图神经网络引导的初始化和自适应变异有效提升了遗传算法在物理互联网库存分销规划问题上的性能，其中学习到的初始化是主要改进来源。该方法为处理不确定成本下的三层网络分配与流协调问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Inventory and distribution planning in Physical Internet networks requires coordinating factory-hub assignments, factory supply, lateral transshipment among collaborative hubs, retailer deliveries, and shortages. The problem combines discrete assignment decisions with interdependent continuous flows, while uncertain operating costs make robust planning more difficult. This study formulates deterministic and min-max regret models for a three-echelon network of factories, hubs, and retailers and develops a graph neural network-guided genetic algorithm (GNN-GA) for the assignment decisions. The GNN estimates hub-specific factory-selection probabilities that are used to construct the initial GA population and adapt mutation according to prediction uncertainty. Each previously unseen candidate assignment is evaluated by solving the remaining continuous-flow problem to LP optimality. Simulated annealing, a standard GA, and GNN-GA are compared on 15 instances using matched random seeds and fixed limits on distinct assignment evaluations. Because the evaluation budgets for test Instances 13-15 are smaller than the nominal population size, these experiments primarily assess the quality of learned initialization rather than multi-generation evolutionary search. A separate 400-evaluation experiment on exact test Instance 13 permits three complete offspring generations and a partial fourth pass, with GNN-GA outperforming GA in all 10 matched runs. Three independently generated exact-solvable instances provide a separate test of transfer. Ablation results show that learned initialization provides most of the improvement, while entropy-guided mutation has a smaller, instance-dependent effect. Per-instance solution times include GNN inference and search but exclude model training and one-time model setup.

</details>


### [214] [Multitask Pareto Optimization for Monotone Submodular Problems with Dynamic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10425)
*Liam Wigney,Frank Neumann*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究进化多任务框架下的单调子模优化问题，其中所有任务共享同一目标函数但具有不同的动态背包约束（元素成本均匀）。作者证明该结构导致多任务公式中的帕累托前沿较小，从而可以跨任务共享解。通过严格的运行时间分析，证明了所提算法能在期望时间内为每个任务获得(1-1/e)逼近，并通过最大覆盖问题的实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化算法通常独立解决每个优化问题，效率低下。进化多任务处理允许在单次进化运行中同时解决多个相关优化问题，但现有工作缺乏对带约束的子模优化多任务场景的严格理论分析。作者希望通过利用各任务共享目标函数、仅约束不同的结构特性，实现跨任务解共享以提升性能。

Method: 作者针对共享单调子模函数、但具有不同动态背包约束的多任务问题，利用均匀成本假设证明帕累托前沿规模较小。基于此结构特性设计多任务进化算法，使得解可以在不同任务间共享，并使用严格的运行时间分析（期望时间）来评估算法获得(1-1/e)逼近所需的计算开销。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的多任务算法能够在期望时间内为每个任务获得(1-1/e)逼近解。与独立运行的进化解法相比，在特定约束条件下多任务方法可以提升性能。最大覆盖问题的实验进一步验证了理论结论，并展示了不同预算设置下算法的实际行为。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，当多任务共享目标函数且仅约束不同时，均匀成本假设带来的小帕累托前沿性质能够有效促进跨任务解共享，从而为多任务子模优化提供了理论保证和实际可行的算法框架。性能提升取决于约束区域（budget regime）的具体情况。

Abstract: Evolutionary multitasking is a recent approach that solves multiple related optimization problems within a single evolutionary run, rather than addressing each problem separately. We consider monotone submodular optimization problems with dynamic knapsack constraints and study a multitasking formulation in which all tasks share a common monotone submodular function $f$, but differ in their constraints. We focus on the case where elements within each constraint have uniform cost and show that this structure leads to small Pareto fronts in the multitasking formulation. This enables solution sharing across tasks and can improve performance compared to running standard evolutionary approaches independently, depending on the constraint regime. Using rigorous runtime analysis, we analyze the expected time until the proposed multitasking algorithms obtain a $(1 - 1/e)$-approximation for each task. Experimental results for the Maximum Coverage problem complement the theoretical analysis and provide further insight into the practical behavior of the approach across different budget settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Safe Observation Capacity for Opponent Exploitation under Showdown Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09954)
*Jiaxing Guo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对扑克类博弈中因弃牌隐藏手牌导致showing数据非随机缺失的问题，提出了一种"安全主动去删失"(SAD)方法。该方法通过地板安全探针(floor-safe probe)驱动选定的线路进入摊牌(reveal)，利用序列形式流恢复被删失的弃牌质量，并通过安全观测容量κ_ρ(I)刻画修复代价。实验涵盖多种扑克子博弈场景，验证了方法的无条件安全性和条件统计价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扑克类博弈中，玩家弃牌时隐藏了手牌，导致摊牌数据并非随机缺失(MNAR)。传统的逐牌估计器会收敛到有偏的分布，且置信区间随样本量增大反而失去覆盖。这需要一种能安全地主动诱导揭示(de-censoring)的统计方法。

Method: 1) 提出地板安全探针：驱动一条选定线路进入摊牌，揭示所有非弃牌延续，利用序列形式流(sequence-form flow)在"揭示认证历史"(reveal-certified histories)上恢复被删失的弃牌质量。2) 定义安全观测容量κ_ρ(I)：在安全预算ρ下的最大地板安全到达率，其前沿呈凹分段线性，原点斜率等于地板的影子价格。3) 提出SAD(Safe Active De-censoring)算法：结合容量、公共异常路由和稳健部署。4) 推导单次揭示质量因子分解时，条件每目标成本N=Θ̃(1/(κ_ρ(I)πε²))的匹配界。

Result: 在固定棋盘的河牌无分桶子博弈中，构造的公共孪生体承认地板安全响应，价值V=0.815；审计的公共通道仅认证蓝图地板，而总体揭示证据认证至少96%的V。在更广泛的合成对手群体中，公共和求解分组揭示纤维认证安全可利用差距的中位份额分别为73%和91%。独立地板审计覆盖所有评估的探针和响应。

Conclusion: 本文将无条件安全性(floor-safe guarantee)与主动揭示的条件统计价值分离，证明安全的主动揭示策略可以显著恢复被删失的信息，同时保持地板安全保证。路由跨目标的决策仍是启发式的，为未来工作留下空间。

Abstract: In poker-like games, folds hide private cards, so showdown data are missing not at random. The usual per-card estimator converges to a selected distribution, and its confidence sets can lose coverage as the sample grows. A floor-safe probe changes the monitoring process: it drives a chosen line to showdown, reveals every non-fold continuation, and uses sequence-form flow to recover censored fold mass on reveal-certified histories. We price this repair through safe observation capacity $κ_ρ(I)$, the largest floor-safe reach rate at safety budget $ρ$. Its frontier is concave and piecewise linear, with origin slope equal to the floor's shadow price. When one-hand reveal mass factorizes into safe reach and opponent continuation, matching bounds give conditional per-target cost $N=\widetildeΘ(1/(κ_ρ(I)π\varepsilon^2))$ for a local censored-fiber direction. Safe Active De-censoring (SAD) combines capacity with public-anomaly routing and robust deployment; routing across targets remains heuristic. Evidence spans bucketed turn-river endgames, controlled instances, and a fixed-board unbucketed river subgame. In the latter, a constructed public twin admits a floor-safe response of value $V=0.815$; the audited public channel certifies only the blueprint floor, while population reveal evidence certifies at least $96\%$ of $V$. Across a broader synthetic opponent population, public and solved grouped reveal fibers certify median shares of $73\%$ and $91\%$ of the safe-exploitable gap. Independent floor audits cover every evaluated probe and response. The results separate unconditional safety from the conditional statistical value of active reveal.

</details>


### [216] [Efficiency Adjustments Break the Logarithmic Rank Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09984)
*Josue Ortega,Geng Zhao,Gabriel Ziegler*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究EADA机制在i.i.d.匹配市场中的期望平均排名。证明了EADA的期望平均排名最多为4loglogn+O(1)，显著优于DA的对数阶，并将结论推广到更广泛的帕累托有效改进DA的机制类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DA机制虽然策略防范性好，但学生的期望平均排名仅为对数阶，效率较低。EADA作为DA的帕累托有效改进，其期望平均排名的渐近行为尚未被研究，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过理论分析证明EADA的期望平均排名上界为4loglogn+O(1)；进一步利用较弱的上界O((loglogn)^2)将结论推广到所有弱帕累托支配DA的帕累托有效机制；并通过扩展论证覆盖多对一市场（有界配额）和相关偏好随机市场。

Result: EADA的期望平均排名为双对数阶，打破了DA的对数屏障；所有弱帕累托支配DA的帕累托有效机制都能将对数阶改进为（较弱但仍然是）双对数阶；结论在多对一市场和相关偏好市场中依然成立。

Conclusion: EADA及其广义类别（帕累托有效的DA改进机制）在期望平均排名方面具有更优的渐近表现，这是首次为EADA和此类机制提供期望平均排名的渐近保证。

Abstract: We study the expected average rank achieved by the Efficiency-Adjusted Deferred Acceptance (EADA) mechanism in i.i.d.\ matching markets. While student-proposing Deferred Acceptance gives students an expected average rank of logarithmic order, we prove that EADA's expected average rank is at most $4\log\log n+O(1)$. Therefore, EADA improves the asymptotic order of students' assignments.
  At the cost of a weaker bound, $O((\log\log n)^2)$, we extend this conclusion to a much larger class of mechanisms. Namely, every Pareto-efficient mechanism that weakly Pareto-dominates DA breaks DA's logarithmic barrier. These are the first asymptotic guarantees for the expected average rank of EADA and of the broader class of Pareto-efficient improvements of DA. The conclusions extend to many-to-one markets with bounded quotas and random markets with correlated preferences.

</details>


### [217] [Resolving Envy by Adding Goods with Bounded Supply: A Type-Count Dichotomy and Two-Agent Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10326)
*Chuang-Chieh Lin,Guillaume Fertin,Po-An Chen,Stéphane Vialette,Géraldine Jean,Emile Benoist,Colin Cleveland*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在额外物品供给有限且无独立预算约束的情况下，通过添加物品来消除嫉妒（EEAG）的问题。针对二元加性估值，建立了尖锐的类型数量二分法：当只有一种额外物品类型时，问题可在多项式时间内求解（Bellman-Ford算法）；而当有两种额外物品类型时，问题为NP完全问题。此外，即使只有两个代理和相同估值，若物品类型数量增长，问题也是弱NP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEAG问题在公平分配领域具有重要意义，Bentert等人之前的研究中两类型情况尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，同时深入理解供给有限情况下嫉妒消除问题的计算复杂度边界。

Method: 针对单类型情形，将嫉妒约束建模为差分约束系统，利用Bellman-Ford算法求解分量最小的可行扩展。针对两类型情形，通过从已知NP完全问题进行归约来证明复杂性下界；对于两代理情形，构造从子集和问题等弱NP完全问题的归约。

Result: (1) 单额外物品类型时，EEAG多项式时间可解，算法支持任意非负整数每份价值；(2) 恰好两额外物品类型时，即使供给有限且一类物品的批准者是另一类的子集，问题仍为NP完全；(3) 两代理、相同加性估值、一个初始物品、多单位供给物品类型情况下，问题为弱NP完全。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了二元加性估值下EEAG问题的计算复杂度二分法：单类型易解，两类型难解。同时揭示了有界供给下复杂度困难的两种表现形态：两类型多代理，以及两代理多类型。这些结果闭合了Bentert等人留下的公开问题。

Abstract: We study envy elimination by adding goods (EEAG) when the additional pool has bounded supply and no separate budget bound. We establish a sharp type-count dichotomy for binary additive valuations. With one additional item type, EEAG is polynomial-time solvable for any number of agents. More generally, our algorithm permits arbitrary nonnegative integer per-copy values. The envy constraints form a system of difference constraints, and Bellman--Ford returns the componentwise least feasible extension. In contrast, with exactly two additional item types, EEAG is \textsf{NP}-complete even when both types have positive finite supply and the approvers of one type form a subset of the approvers of the other. This closes the two-type case left open by Bentert et al. Separately, we prove weak \textsf{NP}-completeness even for two agents with identical additive valuations, one initially endowed good, and a growing number of unit-supply item types. Thus, bounded-supply hardness appears both with two item-types and many agents and with two agents and many item-types.

</details>


### [218] [The Game of Marginal Utilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10373)
*Isaac M. Sonin,Georgy Gaitsgori,Yaakov Malinovsky*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个非合作资源分配博弈，其中m个玩家将固定资源分配到n个项目上，收益函数结合了收益递减和竞争者造成的拥塞效应。作者证明该博弈存在唯一纳什均衡，并通过等边际原则刻画其特征；发现项目按a_i/b_i排序后，每个玩家投资于项目的初始段，且这些段在玩家间嵌套，均衡可分解为连续的活动区间。在完全活跃状态下，均衡可简化为单个标量非线性方程。此外，作者提出了投影边际效用量算法（具有全局线性收敛性）和结构利用的Block Pandora算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源分配博弈在经济学和运筹学中具有广泛的应用，但现有研究往往未能充分结合收益递减和拥塞效应这两种实际场景中普遍存在的因素。作者旨在建立一个统一的博弈模型，既能捕捉这两种效应，又能得到均衡的结构性刻画和高效算法，从而为实际资源分配问题提供理论基础和计算工具。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了一个m玩家n项目的非合作资源分配博弈，收益函数为分式形式（分母包含自身与竞争者的资源总和），结合了收益递减与拥塞效应。然后通过等边际原则刻画纳什均衡的唯一性，并利用项目排序（按a_i/b_i）揭示均衡的嵌套段结构。在完全活跃状态下，将均衡约简为单个标量非线性方程；同时设计了投影边际效用算法（具有显式步长条件下的全局线性收敛性）和Block Pandora算法（利用嵌套截断结构重建并验证均衡）。

Result: 作者证明博弈存在唯一纳什均衡；均衡具有嵌套段结构，资源较多的玩家在每个项目上的投资弱占优；完全活跃状态下均衡可由单个标量方程求解；提出的投影边际效用算法在显式步长条件下全局线性收敛，Block Pandora算法能够高效重建并验证均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为结合收益递减与拥塞效应的资源分配博弈提供了完整的理论刻画（唯一性、结构特征）和高效的计算方法，既给出了均衡的解析结构（嵌套段与活动区间分解），又提供了可验证的数值算法，兼具理论深度与实用价值。

Abstract: We study a noncooperative resource-allocation game in which $m$ players distribute fixed resources among $n$ projects and the payoff of player $j$ is given by \[ F^j(x) =
\sum_{i=1}^n \frac{a_i x_i^j}{b_i+\sum_{\ell=1}^m x_i^\ell}, \] where $a_i$ and $b_i$ are project parameters, while $x_i^j$ is the amount of resources player $j$ allocates to project $i$. This specification combines diminishing returns with congestion generated by competitors. We prove that the game has a unique Nash equilibrium and characterize it by an equimarginal principle. We show that, after the projects are ordered by $a_i/b_i$, each player invests in an initial segment of projects, and these segments are nested across players, so the equilibrium decomposes into consecutive activity zones; players with larger resources invest weakly more in every project. In the fully active regime, where every player invests in every project, we reduce the equilibrium to a single scalar nonlinear equation for the aggregate marginal-utility rate; all individual marginal rates, investments, and payoffs then follow from explicit formulas. We also provide a projected marginal-utility algorithm with global linear convergence under an explicit step-size condition, together with a structure-exploiting \textit{Block Pandora} algorithm that reconstructs and certifies the equilibrium conditional on a proposed nested cutoff structure.

</details>


### [219] [To EFX OR to MMS, That is the Question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10397)
*Hadi Hosseini,Payas Khurana,Shraddha Pathak,Rohit Vaish*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分割物品的公平分配中，每个智能体必须在EFX（无嫉妒至任意物品）或MMS（最大最小份额）二者之一得到满足的"析取"公平概念。令人惊讶的是，这种灵活性并不总能保证解的存在性：作者构造了三个智能体、八个子模物品（以及七个子模杂务）的反例，显著强化了近期EFX不可能性结果。正面结果方面，证明了至多三种估值类型（其中一种是单例）的可加性混合物品存在性，并给出超越可加性的推广及近似方案。此外还发现了析取与其组成部分之间的清晰分离，并证明相同可加性估值下混合物品甚至满足合取EFX∧MMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFX的存在性本身已困扰学界近十年仍未解决，而MMS在部分场景下也会失败。作者探索一个自然思路：允许每个智能体灵活选择两种公平标准中的任意一种（而非固定一种），看这种"宽松化"能否恢复存在性。这个问题兼具理论与实际意义，因为现实中不同智能体可能偏好不同公平诉求。

Method: 通过构造反例（三个智能体+八个子模商品/七个子模杂务）证明EFX∨MMS析取概念下仍无存在性保证；正面采用构造性证明方法，证明三类估值类型的可加性混合物品（含单例类型）存在解；对同类可加性估值证明更强结论EFX∧MMS；并对商品及三智能体杂务给出近似分配方案。

Result: (1) 构造出三智能体八个子模商品及三智能体七个子模杂务的无解反例；(2) 证明至多三种估值类型（含单例）的可加性混合物品存在EFX∨MMS分配，该证明可推广至非可加商品并给出近似方案；(3) 揭示析取与其组成概念的分歧：两类估值的可加性杂务中EFX和MMS各自失败但析取总成立；(4) 相同可加性估值下混合物品满足更强的EFX∧MMS合取性质。

Conclusion: 允许按智能体灵活选择公平证书（EFX或MMS）确实拓展了公平解的边界，能覆盖许多单独标准无法解决的实例；但这一灵活性仍不足以完全恢复存在性，析取概念本身也面临新的不可能性。研究在正面可能性与负面不可能性之间刻画了该问题的精确前沿。

Abstract: We study the agent-wise disjunction of two central fairness notions for indivisible items, where every agent must be either envy-free up to any item (EFX) or maximin-share (MMS) satisfied. One might expect this flexibility to restore existence, especially because the existence of EFX itself resisted resolution for nearly a decade. Surprisingly, it does not. We construct counterexamples with three agents and eight submodular goods, and with three agents and seven submodular chores, significantly strengthening recent EFX impossibility results. On the positive side, we prove existence for additive mixed items with at most three valuation types when one type is a singleton. Our proof extends beyond additivity for goods and yields approximation schemes for goods and three-agent chores instances. We also identify a clean separation between the disjunction and its constituents: For additive chores with two valuation types, EFX and MMS are both known to fail, whereas an EFX$\vee$MMS allocation always exists. Finally, we show that identical additive valuations even admit the conjunction EFX$\wedge$MMS for mixed items. Overall, our results show that allowing flexibility in choosing agent-specific fairness certificates expands the frontier of fair solutions while also uncovering surprising impossibilities.

</details>


### [220] [Non-Existence of EFX Chore Allocations for Monotone Cost Functions with Binary Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10572)
*Zehan Lin,Shengxin Liu,Biaoshuai Tao,Shengwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有二元边际单调成本的不可分杂务（chores）的EFX（无嫉妒至任意一项）分配问题。作者通过一个基于18个智能体、53个杂务的构造，提供了两个反例，分别针对二元XOS成本和二元超模成本，证明在这两种情况下完整的EFX分配不一定存在，并在Lean 4中形式化验证了主要结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于不可分商品的EFX分配存在性已有肯定答案（针对一般具有二元边际的单调函数），但对于不可分杂务（chores）而言，一般二元边际情形下的EFX分配存在性此前仍悬而未决，仅知道更受限类别的结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过反例否定一般二元边际情形下EFX分配的普遍存在性。

Method: 作者构造了一个基于18个智能体、53个杂务的"word gadget"反例框架，并为两种不同的成本结构（二元XOS成本和二元超模成本）设计了相应的成本配置。通过这两种成本配置，分别展示完整的EFX分配不存在。此外，作者使用Lean 4证明助手对主要结果进行了形式化验证。

Result: 本文提供了两个反例：（1）在二元XOS成本下，完整的EFX分配不需要存在；（2）在二元超模成本下，完整的EFX分配同样不需要存在。两个反例均基于相同的18-agent、53-chore构造。所有主要结果已在Lean 4中形式化并验证。

Conclusion: 与商品情形（一般二元边际下EFX必存在）形成对比，对于杂务（chores），即使在二元XOS和二元超模这两个相对受限的成本类别下，EFX分配也不保证存在。这标志着杂务分配中EFX存在性问题的边界被进一步明确，并通过机器验证确保了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: We study the existence of envy-free up to any item (EFX) allocations of indivisible chores when agents have monotone cost functions with binary marginals. For indivisible goods, the corresponding existence question is known to have an affirmative answer for general monotone functions with binary marginals. For chores, however, the existence of EFX allocation was previously known only for more restricted classes, while the general binary-marginal case remained unresolved. In this paper, we provide two counterexamples based on the same 18-agent, 53-chore word gadget, with one cost profile for binary XOS costs and another for binary supermodular costs. In both cases, a complete EFX allocation need not exist. Finally, we formalize and verify our main results in Lean 4.

</details>


### [221] [A Consolidated Game Framework for Cooperative Defense Against Cross-Domain Cyber Attacks in Satellite-Enabled Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10873)
*Linan Huang,Peilong Liu,Xu Chen,Chunxiao Jiang,Linling Kuang,Jianhua Lu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对卫星物联网跨域网络安全问题，提出三方安全博弈框架，通过流量定价机制激励物联网网络运营商（IoT-NO）阻止恶意数据包渗透至卫星域，并设计高效学习算法使IoT-NO优化流量采样策略、卫星服务提供商（SAT-SP）确定最优流量定价，仿真验证了该框架能有效抵御跨域网络攻击并促进激励不一致的双方协同防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星物联网采用跨域多层架构，易受跨域网络威胁。攻击者可通过受损IoT设备注入恶意数据包，利用软硬件及协议漏洞威胁卫星网络。单域防御效果有限，需要跨域协同防御，但跨域影响量化难、参与者激励不一致，导致系统化协同防御开发困难。

Method: 提出三方安全博弈框架表征地面与卫星域的攻击与防御影响；基于该博弈模型设计流量定价机制最优激励IoT-NO阻止恶意数据包进入卫星域；提出高效学习算法，使IoT-NO学习理想流量采样策略、SAT-SP确定最优流量定价。

Result: 仿真结果验证了该综合博弈框架在对抗跨域网络攻击方面的有效性，并促进了激励不一致的IoT-NO与SAT-SP之间的协同防御。

Conclusion: 三方安全博弈框架结合流量定价机制与学习算法，为解决卫星物联网跨域攻击提供了系统化协同防御方案，有效协调了多方参与者之间的激励偏差。

Abstract: As the adoption of satellite-enabled Internet of Things (IoT) continues to rise, its intricate multidomain architecture becomes increasingly susceptible to cross-domain cyber threats. Attackers can exploit compromised IoT devices, inject malicious packets into data streams aggregated at the IoT gateway for satellite backhaul, and potentially endanger the satellite network during transmission by exploiting the hardware, software, and protocol vulnerabilities. Compared to single-domain defenses, cooperative defense at the IoT devices, IoT access network, and satellite transmission network provides fine-granularity defense against cross-domain intelligent attacks. However, quantifying cross-domain impacts and tilting incentive misalignment among different participants remain significant challenges, making systematic cooperative defense development a complex task. To address this, we develop a tripartite security game framework to characterize the impacts of attacks and defense methods across both the terrestrial and satellite domains. Leveraging this game model, we devise flow pricing to optimally motivate the IoT network operator (IoT-NO) to prevent malicious packet infiltration into the satellite domain. Subsequently, we propose efficient learning algorithms enabling both the IoT-NO to ascertain their ideal flow sampling strategies and the satellite service provider (SAT-SP) to determine optimal flow pricing. The simulation results corroborate the effectiveness of the consolidated game in counteracting cross-domain cyber attacks and facilitating cooperative defense between the IoT-NO and the SAT-SP with nonaligned incentives.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Optimize Cheap, Deploy Strong: Cost-Aware Cross-Tier Transfer for Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10694)
*Tal Oved,Roi Pony,Oshri Naparstek,Udi barzelay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过解耦LLM在进化搜索中的三个角色（评估、反思/变异、部署），将高流量的评估角色放在最便宜的模型层，仅对稀有的反思/变异算子使用强模型，并利用跨层迁移将廉价搜索得到的提示词部署到更强的目标模型上，从而大幅降低提示词和智能体程序的进化优化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM提示词和智能体程序的进化优化（如GEPA）主要由适应度评估主导：评估每个候选方案需要在验证集上运行一个问答LLM，因此评估者的价格层级决定了总搜索成本。现有方法忽略了不同角色所需的模型能力差异，导致成本浪费。

Method: 将LLM在搜索中扮演的三个角色解耦：高容量的回答/评估角色放在最便宜的模型层，强模型仅用于稀有的反思/变异算子；利用向上跨层迁移，将廉价层搜索出的提示词部署到更强的目标模型上；并提供成本受限的刻画，分析廉价层搜索何时可替代目标层搜索、何时失败。

Result: 在四个任务（HotpotQA、IFBench、LiveBench-Math、HoVer）和四个模型家族的11个模型上，所提出的提示词匹配或超过同层优化，同时将96%以上的搜索token放在最便宜的层，搜索成本降低5.6-14倍；在推理层每次适应度调用都产生长思维链的场景下，成本降低可达25-54倍。

Conclusion: 通过角色解耦和跨层迁移，可以在不损失优化质量的前提下大幅降低进化搜索成本，为LLM提示词和智能体程序的低成本优化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Evolutionary optimization of LLM prompts and agentic programs (e.g., GEPA) is dominated by fitness evaluation: scoring each candidate runs an answering LLM over a validation set, so the evaluator's price tier dictates total search cost. We restructure that search by decoupling the three roles an LLM plays, running the high-volume answering role on the cheapest tier, reserving a strong model for the rare reflection/variation operator, then exploiting upward cross-tier transfer to deploy the cheaply evolved prompt on a stronger target. We contribute a cost-controlled characterization of when cheap-tier search substitutes for target-tier search, and where it fails. Across four tasks (HotpotQA, IFBench, LiveBench-Math, HoVer) and eleven models in four model families, the resulting prompt matches or exceeds same-tier optimization while placing over 96% of search tokens on the cheapest tier, at 5.6-14x lower search cost, rising to 25-54x where reasoning tiers emit long chains of thought on every fitness call.

</details>


### [223] [Detecting an Effect Is Not Learning to Act on It: A Reward-SNR Floor for LLM Acquisition Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10441)
*Ying Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了一个关键区分：检测辅助信号（如LLM推理、慢速oracle、昂贵测量）在平均意义上有帮助，与学习在逐实例层面何时使用该信号，是两回事。作者提出奖励信噪比（reward-SNR）下限理论，证明当SNR低于阈值ρ*(N)≈2.8/√N时，任何可部署的逐实例采集策略都无法被离线学习，即使in-sample oracle显示显著增益，该增益也仅仅是噪声的阶统计量。作者以结构化假设嵌入（SHE）为具体实例，在三个真实数据集上验证了这一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 很多pipeline可以付费获取辅助的模型衍生观测信号（如LLM的结构化推理、慢速oracle、昂贵测量），但必须决定何时使用这些信号。现有方法混淆了两个概念：平均意义上信号有帮助 vs. 在每实例上学习使用信号。作者发现存在一个被忽视的奖励SNR下限，决定后者是否可能实现，即使信号忠实且in-sample oracle展示出可观的表面增益。

Method: 1) 理论分析：提出reward-SNR可检测性下限ρ*(N)≈2.8/√N，证明路由策略只有在该阈值以上才可离线估计。2) 实证验证：跨per-impression、cluster、regime、uplift-tree多种粒度测试学习路由，发现均不优于随机；用matched-moment噪声placebo复制了≥100%的oracle表面增益，证明"可学习结构"实为噪声的阶统计量。3) 引入SHE（Structured Hypothesis Embeddings）：用冻结LLM将用户历史转化为排序的、带置信度评分和证据支撑的意图假设，融合进推荐系统。

Result: 在MIND、REES46、Amazon-Beauty三个公开数据集上，SHE显示忠实性和可校准性，但其价值高度依赖backbone和regime（在有序GRU上显著提升+0.0114，95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209]，但在全局冗余差距上与零不可区分）。所有三个数据集的SNR均低于下限，因此任何粒度的学习采集策略都崩塌。正对照实验确认这是真实的低SNR极限而非pipeline故障。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：可靠的单元是设计时的regime gate（何时在系统层面启用信号），而非逐实例的采集策略。当数据SNR低于下限时，oracle显示的表观增益是噪声的阶统计量，不可被任何部署策略利用。这项工作提醒研究者警惕将in-sample oracle增益误认为可学习的策略空间。

Abstract: Many pipelines can pay a per-example cost to acquire an auxiliary, model-derived observation -- an LLM's structured reasoning, a slow oracle, an expensive measurement -- and then must decide when the acquired signal is worth using. Our thesis is a distinction that is easy to miss: detecting that such a signal helps on average is not the same as learning to act on it per instance, and a reward-SNR floor governs when the second is even possible. Even when the signal is faithful and an in-sample oracle picking the top-b examples by realized reward shows a sizable apparent gain, no deployable policy can learn when to acquire it: across per-impression, cluster, regime, and uplift-tree granularities, learned routing never beats random, and a matched-moment noise placebo reproduces >=100% of the oracle's apparent gain -- the apparent "learnable structure" is order statistics of noise. We explain this with one distinction, detecting a mean effect vs. learning a per-instance acquisition policy, and a reward-SNR detectability floor: routing is estimable offline only if the reward SNR rho clears rho*(N) ~= 2.8/sqrt(N), with a positive control confirming a true low-SNR limit rather than a broken pipeline. As a concrete instantiation we introduce Structured Hypothesis Embeddings (SHE): a frozen LLM turns a user history into ranked, confidence-scored, evidence-grounded intent hypotheses, fused into a recommender. On three public datasets (MIND, REES46, Amazon-Beauty), SHE is faithful and calibratable, yet its value is backbone- and regime-conditional (significant over an ordered GRU, +0.0114, 95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209], but a global redundancy gap indistinguishable from zero), and learned acquisition collapses at every granularity because all three datasets sit below the floor. The realizable unit is a design-time regime gate, not a per-instance policy. We release code and a one-command reproduction.

</details>


### [224] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV，一个基于持续同调（Persistent Homology）的拓扑框架，用于分析Transformer表示在层间前向传播过程中的演化规律，通过构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形来追踪表示点云的全局拓扑特征如何逐步从原始输入演化为任务相关的特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表示，缺乏对单个表示及其流形在层间演化过程的深入探索。理解"Transformer如何学习"这一核心问题需要揭示原始输入表示何时以及如何演化为任务相关特征表示的具体过程和关键层。

Method: TGO-IV利用持续同调构建拓扑分析框架：从token级表示点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并采用多种互补的拓扑观测工具（包括Persistence Diagrams、Barcode Diagrams、Betti Curves、Persistence Landscapes、Bottleneck Distance和Wasserstein Distance）来全面刻画各层表示点云全局拓扑的演化特征。

Result: 该框架能够综合多种拓扑不变量，实现对Transformer各层表示点云全局拓扑演化的全面分析，从而有助于识别关键层或建立趋势，以揭示原始输入表示向任务相关特征表示的转变过程。

Conclusion: TGO-IV为Transformer表示演化提供了全新的拓扑视角，超越了局部几何性质的分析，有望在隔离关键层或确认演化趋势方面提供更接近"Transformer如何学习"这一根本问题的见解。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [225] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于直觉模糊理论的深度随机向量函数链接网络（IF-dRVFL 和 IF-edRVFL），通过联合考虑隶属度与非隶属度为训练样本赋予自适应权重，有效区分干净样本、噪声样本和异常值，从而提升模型在含噪真实数据集上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度随机神经网络（如 dRVFL 和 edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁，缺乏对噪声和异常值的区分能力，导致在真实数据集上鲁棒性不足；此外，污染特征在隐藏层间的传播会进一步削弱决策能力。因此需要一种能自适应加权样本的鲁棒框架。

Method: 将直觉模糊理论引入 dRVFL 和 edRVFL 框架中。在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，同时计算每个样本的隶属度（基于样本到类中心的距离）和非隶属度（基于局部邻域内样本的异质性），进而为训练样本赋予自适应权重，实现对干净、噪声和异常样本的有效判别。

Result: 在 UCI 和 KEEL 基准数据集上进行了大量实验（包括有无高斯噪声的场景），结果表明所提出的 IF-dRVFL 和 IF-edRVFL 模型在性能上优于现有的 SOTA 模糊与非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 直觉模糊理论能有效提升深度随机神经网络的鲁棒性，自适应样本加权策略能够更好应对真实数据中的噪声与异常值干扰，且代码已开源供复现。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [226] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurveFP，一种闭环乘积码本家族，通过在紧凑块尺度下将量化幅度分布在交错的对数曲线上，实现低精度数据类型下的算术协同设计。CurveFP使乘积运算变为精确的符号异或和整数索引更新，显著简化了乘积路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低精度数据类型大多优化标量保真度，但由其乘积引发的算术操作保持不变，这限制了低精度训练与推理的效率提升。

Method: 提出闭环乘积码本家族CurveFP：采用交错的对数曲线分布量化幅度，以有理基数调整动态范围与局部分辨率的权衡；均匀曲线索引确保每个非零乘积在代数上封闭，乘积形成变为精确的符号异或和整数索引更新；推导出的有限相位数决定累加调度。实例化两个版本：CurveFP eight（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练，CurveFP seven（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP seven在4个7B-9B模型上以少1个元素比特击败张量级FP8困惑度，且与原生质量差距在1.32%以内；CurveFP eight在全部36个配对前向和后向GEMM对比中降低操作数NMSE；在3个匹配的128.3M参数三元组中完成3B-token预训练，BF16推理困惑度22.5366优于FP8的22.5407；36格下游矩阵中CurveFP eight训练检查点在全部12个种子-格式对比中取得更低的WikiText-103困惑度。

Conclusion: CurveFP建立了一种算术协同设计范式，同时具备FP8级别的数值行为、七比特推理能力以及显著简化的乘积路径，为低精度语言模型训练与部署提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [227] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SFRL（Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning），一个面向异构联邦系统的通用表征学习框架，通过可学习的sheaf限制映射和流形约束的几何对齐正则项，在不假设共享全局潜在空间的情况下实现异构智能体间的表征对齐，并配套提出Sheaf-FRL去中心化算法，证明其在确定性和随机场景下收敛到一阶稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统中，各智能体在数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度和学习目标上均存在差异，传统联邦学习方法通常假设存在共享的全局潜在空间，这限制了其在异构场景下的表现。

Method: SFRL通过可学习的sheaf限制映射，结合正交变换和等距嵌入实现相邻潜在表征的对齐，并由sheaf拉普拉斯诱导的二次粘合正则项来强制对齐，该正则项在共享的少量pilot样本上计算以保证可扩展性和通信效率。配套的Sheaf-FRL算法交替进行局部模型的梯度更新和边限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新。

Result: 在语义通信的协作分类任务中（存在模型和数据异构），Sheaf-FRL在局部分类精度和后通信分类精度上均优于基线方法，且对不同水平的局部分布偏移表现出更好的鲁棒性，对潜在空间维度压缩也具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SFRL是一种无需共享全局潜在空间的异构联邦表征学习方法，通过几何对齐实现全局一致性的涌现，理论保证收敛性，实验验证了在异构和通信受限场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [228] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体工具文档的信息字段进行了大规模实证研究，发现不同信息字段的效果高度依赖任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式，并提出自适应工具文档优化框架DocsChisel，通过分析失败轨迹迭代增删改信息字段，实验显示比原始文档提升95.89%成功率，比现有基线平均提升75.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，但将工具文档视为固定输入。虽然近期有工作尝试重写或压缩文档，但对文档所含信息如何影响智能体性能知之甚少，缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究，揭示工具文档信息字段的异质性及其对性能的条件依赖。在此基础上提出DocsChisel框架：分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹以识别文档相关问题，并通过添加、移除、精炼每个工具的信息字段来迭代优化工具文档。

Result: DocsChisel将LLM智能体任务成功率比原始文档提升95.89%，比现有基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时保持有限的优化时间和token开销。

Conclusion: 固定的工具文档无法在不同智能体设置下持续泛化，自适应针对每个工具优化文档信息字段能显著提升智能体性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [229] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowScout是一个执行引导的框架，能从历史任务解决方案记录中自动生成集成真实工具调用的智能体工作流，通过挖掘工具协调骨架并用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈进行优化，在工具调用正确性和执行质量上大幅超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成智能体工作流的方法主要产生以LLM为中心的工作流，其中真实的工具执行被抽象并模拟为LLM节点，限制了生成工作流的可用性和稳定性。作者旨在解决这一局限，生成真正集成工具执行的工作流。

Method: FlowScout将智能体工作流表示为由LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边组成的有向图。首先从历史记录中挖掘通用的工具协调骨架来构建初始工作流，然后通过蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个代表性任务域上与PM4Py、ReAct和AFlow三个基线相比，FlowScout生成的智能体工作流在工具调用正确性上至少提升92.69%，执行质量至少提升17.66%，且重复运行时的性能波动更低。

Conclusion: FlowScout验证了通过执行反馈引导的搜索方法可以生成更可靠、更稳定且真正集成工具调用的智能体工作流，为自动工作流生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [230] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UserToolBench基准，用于评估工具使用大语言模型在个性化决策方面的能力——即从用户交互历史中推断潜在偏好、识别何时需要澄清，并在信息不完整的情况下生成符合用户意图的工具调用轨迹。实验表明现有模型在个性化委托任务上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用LLM的基准大多只关注个人资料回忆、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级个性化，缺乏对个性化决策制定的评估。当LLM代表用户执行任务时，需要真正理解用户潜在偏好并做出正确决策，而不仅仅是生成听起来个性化的输出。

Method: 构建UserToolBench基准：基于隐私脱敏的真实交互轨迹，结合结构化人物画像、公开API风格工具生态系统和长时程多轮轨迹。包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1065轮对话、170个独特工具，任务类型覆盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具场景。

Result: 强工具使用LLM的实验表明，当前模型在个性化委托任务上仍存在困难。多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长时程行为一致性是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应超越"输出是否听起来像用户"的标准，转向"LLM是否为其代表的用户做出正确决策"。这为未来个性化LLM评估提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [231] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从成对比较中联合学习物品奖励与工人胜任度（可靠性）的问题。作者采用Boltzmann-rational模型（扩展Bradley-Terry-Luce模型），通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转化为条件高斯形式，推导出基于EM的算法，并将问题简化为矩阵感知问题，从而获得理论收敛保证。实验表明该算法在真实与合成数据上优于多种基线，并对垃圾工人和对抗性工人具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在众包平台上（如Amazon Mechanical Turk）收集的成对比较数据往往包含不可靠的工人，他们可能因领域知识不足或追求收益最大化（刷屏）而提供低质量标签。现有研究多假定工人可靠，但现实场景中工人不可靠性是普遍存在的。因此作者希望探究是否可以联合学习工人胜任度与物品奖励，以提高奖励学习的准确性。

Method: 采用Boltzmann-rational模型（BT-Luce模型的扩展），将工人胜任度纳入成对比较的建模中。引入Polya-Gamma潜变量，将逻辑似然变换为条件高斯形式，使得EM算法的E-step中Q函数得以简化。通过这种变换，将学习问题归结为矩阵感知（matrix sensing）问题，从而利用矩阵恢复理论建立算法的收敛性保证。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上进行了广泛实验，实验结果表明所提出的算法在多个基线上具有优势，并且对垃圾工人和对抗性工人表现出强大的鲁棒性，验证了该方法在现实众包和奖励学习场景中的实用有效性。代码和数据已公开。

Conclusion: 作者证明了工人胜任度与物品奖励可以联合学习，所提出的基于Polya-Gamma变分技术的EM算法不仅在理论上具有收敛保证，在实际的众包比较数据中也表现优异且鲁棒，为不可靠工人环境下的偏好学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [232] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从供给侧（候选人）视角研究ML工程中的软技能，使用基于LLM的流水线从300份精选简历中提取显式列出和隐式叙述的软技能，并通过13个可证伪假设检验供给侧文献的结论。发现11个假设得到支持、1个部分支持、1个被推翻：候选人主要通过叙述而非关键词列表披露软技能（约3:1），领导力、协调和指导等核心能力尤其如此（88-96%为叙述）。年资将领导力表达几率提高近三倍，而软件工程师表述领导力的比率仅为同行一半，基于关键词的筛选系统性地遗漏了技术候选人的软技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ML工程软技能的知识几乎全部来自需求侧（招聘广告、调查、招聘经理访谈），供给侧候选人如何表述这些能力从未被研究。已有简历挖掘工作存在两个缺陷：基于关键词无法捕捉叙述性技能，且仅为描述性频率排名，未检验组间差异是否超过抽样变异。

Method: 构建300份覆盖三类角色的均衡简历语料库，使用基于LLM的流水线（针对人工标注的地面真值验证）提取显式列出和隐式叙述的软技能，这是现有提取器无法区分的。将需求侧文献的结论转化为13个关于角色特征、年资进展和披露风格的可证伪假设，使用效应量并在家族误差控制下检验。

Result: 11个假设得到支持，1个部分支持，1个被推翻。候选人通过叙述而非关键词列表披露软技能约3:1，雇主最重视的能力（领导力、协调、指导）叙述比例高达88-96%。年资使领导力表述几率提高近三倍。领导力并非如先前假设所认为的普遍能力——软件工程师表述比率仅为同行一半。

Conclusion: 技术候选人确实会表述软技能，但基于关键词的筛选会系统性地遗漏它们。供给侧数据既能印证也能反驳需求侧文献的结论，而不仅仅是简单说明，这凸显了双视角研究软技能的必要性。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [233] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述212项研究，调查了物理学知识嵌入机器学习（PIML）在预测与健康管理（PHM）领域的应用情况，提出了四类分类方案（观测偏差、归纳偏差、学习偏差和混合方法），发现PIML方法在各类资产上普遍优于传统基线，但文献严重偏向锂电池和轴承，且缺乏对部分声称优势的充分证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM应用中存在泛化能力差、无法推断因果关系、缺乏可解释性等固有限制。PIML通过将先验物理知识纳入ML流程来缓解这些问题，引起了越来越多的关注，因此需要对这一新兴领域进行系统性梳理和评估。

Method: 对212项研究进行系统文献综述，提出一个四类分类方案（observational bias、inductive bias、learning bias和hybrid approaches），并进一步按PHM任务对研究进行分类分析。

Result: 所有四类PIML方法在广泛的资产类型上均一致表现出优于传统基线的预测性能，但文献主要集中在锂电池和轴承领域，且以特定问题的解决方案为主。关于PIML在泛化性、因果推断和可解释性等限制方面的改进主张缺乏充分证据。

Conclusion: PIML在PHM中已显示出切实的效益，但未来研究应优先关注可迁移的设计模式、集成策略的基准比较，以及适用于在线部署的轻量级、鲁棒的、不确定性感知模型。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [234] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAR-PL（协变量调整残差策略学习）方法，用于从观察性会计日志中为中小企业金融指导进行策略排序，实验表明在85,078条公司月观测数据上，不同方法在不同KPI指标上各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时的金融指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择且经常共同发生的商业变更，而非随机化建议，因此需要从观察性数据中学习策略排序。

Method: 提出CAR-PL（Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning），一种基于action-wise R-learner的方法，直接操作多热日志并通过观察支持进行正则化选择。与uplift T-Learner、保守上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化参考方法进行比较。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利点估计最高(0.084)，T-Learner在收入点估计最高(0.085)，上下文价值模型在速动比率点估计最高(0.062)。在匹配的公司聚类比较中，CAR-PL和T-Learner在增长KPI上无统计显著差异，但CAR-PL选择34个类别且选择分布更不集中。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持从多动作会计日志中进行目标特定排序的中小企业金融指导，不同KPI目标应选择不同的最优策略学习方法。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [235] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoSSM，一个联合建模事件和时间戳的自回归状态空间模型。通过共享骨干网络同时训练token生成和时间生成目标，实验表明联合训练比两阶段训练能产生更具时间信息量的表征，且不会系统性降低内容生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型通常只关注事件预测而忽视时间信息，两阶段方法（先训练事件预测模型，再用冻结表征训练时间模型）隐含假设事件预测优化出的表征已包含足够的时间结构，这一假设可能不成立，限制了时间推理和异常检测等能力。

Method: 提出ChronoSSM，一种自回归状态空间模型（SSM），通过共享骨干网络联合优化token生成目标和时间生成目标，实现事件与时间戳的联合建模。

Result: 在四个领域（涵盖密集和部分时间戳监督）的实验中，联合训练一致性地使冻结表征中更容易恢复出到达间隔信息，且没有系统性损害内容生成质量。

Conclusion: 时间监督能产生更具时间信息量的表征，同时不会实质性削弱自回归事件建模能力，验证了联合建模时序信息与事件内容的价值。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [236] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh,Karthik Kamuju,Kummari Avinash,John Joshua,Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准RLHF通过偏好平均化聚合异质偏好，导致程序公平性失败（多数群体主导奖励学习、少数偏好被系统性忽略），并提出偏好感知RLHF（PA-RLHF）在奖励学习阶段分离不同偏好模式进行优化，在受控实验中将对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最佳与最差对齐群体间的公平差距从15.9个百分点降至9.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF假设偏好同质性，将异质偏好聚合成单一奖励模型，这会导致程序公平性失败：多数偏好群体主导奖励学习，而少数偏好被系统性低估。作者旨在揭示并解决对齐过程中的结构性公平性问题。

Method: 引入偏好感知RLHF（PA-RLHF），在奖励学习阶段而非偏好聚合阶段，将优化过程按不同偏好模式分离（separating optimization across preference modes），从而在奖励建模层面保留不同的偏好信号。

Result: 在受控、无噪声环境中，PA-RLHF将整体对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，同时将最佳与最差对齐群体之间的公平差距从15.9个百分点缩小至9.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 对齐过程中的程序公平性失败可能源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，即便在受控、无噪声条件下也会出现。这对大语言模型和智能体系统有直接影响，因为偏斜的奖励模型会在序列决策中放大不平等。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>


### [237] [SeFoRA: Sketch-Aggregated Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Heterogeneous Client Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10144)
*Yue Xia,Tayyebeh Jahani-Nezhad,Mayank Bakshi,Rawad Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeFoRA，一种基于线性草图聚合的联邦LoRA算法，解决了联邦参数高效微调中客户端使用不同LoRA秩导致的维度不兼容和双线性错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LoRA与联邦PEFT结合时面临两个单独场景不存在的新挑战：1) 客户端可能使用不同的LoRA秩，导致因子矩阵维度不兼容；2) 因子级平均存在双线性错配问题。

Method: SeFoRA让每个客户端传输其本地更新的线性草图，使得联邦聚合器能够直接聚合，缓解双线性错配问题，并允许在全模型的小子空间中聚合。同时提出秩同质版本SeFoRA-Ho，允许该设置下的直接适配器聚合。

Result: 在秩同质设置下证明了以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶驻点的邻域。在GLUE数据集上微调RoBERTa-Large的实验表明算法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeFoRA通过草图聚合有效解决了联邦LoRA中的维度不兼容和双线性错配问题，并有理论收敛保证和实验验证。

Abstract: We consider federated parameter efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks with low-rank adaptation (LoRA,~Hu et al.\ 2022). Combining LoRA with federated PEFT introduces challenges absent from either setting alone: clients may use different LoRA ranks, making their factor matrices dimension-incompatible, and factor-wise averaging suffers from a bilinear mismatch. We propose SeFoRA, a sketch-aggregated federated LoRA algorithm in which each client transmits a linear sketch of its local updates, enabling direct aggregation at the federator. As a result, SeFoRA alleviates the bilinear mismatch, and allows for aggregation in a small subspace of the full model. We introduce a rank-homogeneous version called SeFoRA-Ho which allows for direct adapter aggregation in this setting. We prove convergence to a neighborhood of the first-order stationary point at rate $\cO(1/T)$ for the rank-homogeneous setting. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning RoBERTa-Large on GLUE datasets show how our algorithms outperform the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [238] [The Evaluation Protocol Determines the Result: An Independent Reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10145)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是对LeWorldModel（潜在世界模型）论文的可复现性研究。作者通过独立重实现，在约25美元的云算力上复现了87%的目标达成率（达到94.0%），但发现该结果依赖于论文未披露的四个关键实现约定。此外，论文的评估协议本身存在矛盾（附录与配置文件规定了不同的目标偏移和步数预算），导致同一检查点的结果在84%与14%之间大幅波动。研究发现两个普适性结论：一步预测精度无法预测长时域规划成功；批归一化层可将验证损失放大至300倍，掩盖了训练损失的停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LeWorldModel论文结果的可复现性，揭示论文声称结果与实际实现配置之间的差距，并探讨世界模型评估协议中的潜在问题。

Method: 独立重实现LeWorldModel，使用约25美元出租算力进行训练；严格按已发布的配置文件进行复现对照实验；系统性地改变评估协议中的关键参数（目标偏移、步数预算、目标构建方式）来测试结果的敏感性；并分析预测精度与规划成功、批归一化对损失度量的影响。

Result: 重实现达到94.0%目标达成率（超过作者声称的87%）；但仅用已发布配置无法收敛。作者自身检查点在作者自己的评估协议下为84%，而在论文附录指定的协议下仅为14%。改变目标构建方式（不变其他条件）使同一检查点从84%骤降至8%。预测精度与长时域规划成功无关，批归一化造成验证损失虚高300倍。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel的核心结果严重依赖于未记录的实现细节和评估协议选择，论文报告的数字在协议微调下极不稳定。这凸显了世界模型研究中需要更严格的评估标准化和更完整的实现披露。
